In developing the UID and judge pipeline, we are able to demonstrate 43\% increased event recovery, a framework competitive with clinician annotations, and a system with occurrence-level provenance for both reconstruction and evaluation.

</details>


### [276] [Autonomous Research for Open-Ended Problems: A Case Study on Telecom Ticket Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13073)
*Junghyun Min,Huseyin Uzunalioglu,Mohamed Trabelsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以电信工单检索这一开放式工业级机器学习问题为案例，探索自主研究（autonomous research）在开放式ML问题上的适用性。结果显示：自主研究擅长窄范围超参优化，但缺乏人类直觉与创造力，需额外运维成本；在极少人工监督下可在更短时间内以较低成本达到SOTA的90%性能（Recall@1 0.34 vs 0.38，10周 vs 10月，单次Cursor campaign成本≤$200）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主ML研究框架虽已有提出，但成功案例多局限于搜索空间狭窄的问题（如语言建模或生物医学ML基准）。本文关注自主研究能否推广到开放式、工业级ML问题，以检验其通用性与实际落地能力。

Method: 选取"电信工单检索"作为开放式工业级ML案例研究，该任务在表示、架构和训练数据生成上均存在自由度。使用商业与开源智能体开展自主研究，并与人类研究工作在性能、时间与成本等维度进行对比。

Result: 自主研究在窄范围超参优化上表现优异，但缺乏类人的直觉与创造力，且需要额外的运维开销。在极少人工监督下，自主研究可达到SOTA的90%（Recall@1 0.34 vs 0.38），耗时从人类工作的10个月缩短至10周，成本适中（单次Cursor campaign最高约$200）。

Conclusion: 该实证结果表明，自主研究在开放式工业级ML问题上兼具潜力与局限。建议人类研究者与自主研究框架协同工作，以获得ML研究的最佳效果。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in LLM-based systems and their abilities in problem solving and coding have allowed progress in the AI for Science paradigm, potentially replacing human roles in machine learning (ML) research. However, while several frameworks of fully autonomous end-to-end ML research have been proposed, successful implementations of them are often limited to problems with narrow search spaces, like language modeling or biomedical ML benchmarks. In this paper, we explore how autonomous research can be adapted to solve open-ended, industry-grade ML problems, by considering a case study: telecom ticket retrieval, an open-ended task with degrees of freedom in representation, architecture, and training data generation. We discover that autonomous research for open-ended problems with commercial and open-source agents shows both promise and limitations: while autonomous research can excel in narrow hyperparameter optimization, it lacks human-like intuition and creativity and requires operational overhead. Even with minimal human supervision, autonomous research can reach $90\%$ of state-of-the-art performance (0.34 vs. 0.38 Recall@1) in a much shorter time period (10 weeks vs. 10 months of human work) at a modest cost (up to \$200 per Cursor campaign). Our empirical evidence recommends that human researchers and autonomous research frameworks work together for best results in ML research.

</details>


### [277] [Embodied-BenchForge: A Closed-Loop Agentic Workflow for Embodied Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13082)
*Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Zhe Ji,Jinshan Lai,Xi Ren,Danyang Li,Zheng Yang,Jianwei Hu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Embodied-BenchForge 智能体框架，将用户评估意图转化为完整具身基准，通过闭环合成（前向合成+后向验证修复）解决中间产物缺陷传播问题，构建了6个离线EQA基准和1个含220任务的交互式基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基准构建方法大多只覆盖孤立阶段，或局限于预定义环境和任务族；且多步构建产生的依赖型中间产物常未经特定验证直接传递下游，导致局部缺陷传播到最终基准。

Method: 将构建形式化为闭环基准合成，融合前向产物合成与后向验证修复。技能编排产物合成将类型化、可复用技能组合为可执行工作流，并用产物依赖图记录中间输出及依赖；需求引导的验证与修复在构建全程应用产物特定契约，并利用溯源信息在验证失败时触发局部重执行或上游回滚。

Result: 在离线EQA赛道构建6个覆盖多样具身场景的基准，在交互式具身赛道构建含220个可执行任务的基准；对代表性MLLM和具身智能体的评估表明基准能区分模型在观测理解与闭环执行上的能力；质量评估与消融验证了基准质量及验证修复的有效性，修复与技能复用分析显示高效的局部恢复与跨基准复用性。

Conclusion: Embodied-BenchForge 通过闭环合成与产物级验证机制，实现了从评估意图到完整具身基准的自动化构建，提升了基准质量与构建效率，并具备良好的可复用性。

Abstract: Agentic systems offer a promising way to automate embodied benchmark construction, but existing approaches typically cover isolated stages or remain specialized to predefined environments and task families. More importantly, multi-step construction produces dependent intermediate artifacts that are often passed downstream without artifact-specific verification, allowing local defects to propagate into the final benchmark. We present Embodied-BenchForge, an agentic framework that transforms user-specified evaluation intents into complete embodied benchmark artifacts. It formulates construction as Closed-Loop Benchmark Synthesis, integrating forward artifact synthesis with backward verification and repair. Skill-Orchestrated Artifact Synthesis composes typed and reusable skills into executable workflows, while an artifact dependency graph records intermediate outputs and their dependencies. Requirement-Guided Verification and Repair applies artifact-specific contracts throughout construction and uses provenance to trigger local re-execution or upstream rollback when verification fails. Embodied-BenchForge constructs six benchmarks covering diverse embodied scenarios in the Offline EQA Track, together with one interactive benchmark containing 220 executable tasks in the Interactive Embodied Track. Evaluations of representative MLLMs and embodied agents show that the benchmarks distinguish model capabilities in both observation-based understanding and closed-loop execution. Quality assessment and ablations validate benchmark quality and the effectiveness of verification and repair, while repair and skill-reuse analyses demonstrate efficient localized recovery and cross-benchmark reusability.

</details>


### [278] [CMA-OT: Hierarchical Expert Supervision for Dance-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13118)
*Jinting Wang,Chenxing Li,Dong Yu,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CMA-OT范式，利用外部音乐专家提供分层监督，通过课程引导的多尺度学习与尺度感知最优传输对齐，解决舞蹈到音乐生成中的语义鸿沟问题，实现最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 舞蹈到音乐（D2M）生成旨在合成与舞蹈视频节奏和风格一致的配乐。核心挑战在于：稀疏的舞蹈线索（节奏、风格）与音乐创作所需的密集信息（结构、配器、表现力动态）之间存在语义鸿沟。现有方法仅依赖这些稀疏线索且只监督最终音频输出，导致音乐表征学习不佳，生成的音乐在音乐性和结构连贯性上受限。

Method: 提出CMA-OT（课程引导的多尺度表征对齐结合尺度感知最优传输）。核心思路是引入外部音乐专家为生成器的潜在特征提供分层监督以弥合语义鸿沟。具体包含两方面：一是课程引导的多尺度学习策略，将音乐知识从专家渐进式迁移至音乐生成器，实现稳定有效的表征学习；二是尺度感知最优传输对齐机制，对分层专家表征与生成器潜在特征之间的软对应关系进行建模，以适应不同专家尺度的语义与结构差异，并在时序错位下实现细粒度对齐。

Result: 在两个数据集上的大量实验表明，CMA-OT在节奏同步、感知质量和整体音乐生成方面均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入外部音乐专家的分层监督并结合课程引导的多尺度学习与尺度感知最优传输对齐，CMA-OT有效弥合了稀疏舞蹈线索与密集音乐信息之间的语义鸿沟，显著提升了音乐表征学习与生成音乐的音乐性及结构连贯性。

Abstract: Dance-to-music (D2M) generation aims to synthesize music that is rhythmically and stylistically aligned with dance videos. A key challenge arises from the semantic mismatch between sparse dance cues, such as rhythm and style, and the dense information required for music composition, including structure, instrumentation, and expressive dynamics. Existing methods typically rely on these sparse cues and supervise only the final audio output, resulting in poorly learned music representations and generated music with limited musicality and structural coherence. To address these issues, we propose Curriculum-guided Multi-scale representation Alignment with scale-aware Optimal Transport (CMA-OT), a novel paradigm that leverages an external music expert to provide hierarchical supervision for the generator's latent features, bridging the semantic gap and enhancing representation learning. To effectively incorporate hierarchical supervision, we introduce a curriculum-guided multi-scale learning strategy that progressively transfers musical knowledge from the expert to the music generator, enabling stable and effective representation learning. Moreover, to accommodate the semantic and structural variations across different expert scales and achieve fine-grained alignment under temporal mismatch, we propose a scale-aware optimal transport alignment mechanism, which models soft correspondences between hierarchical expert representations and the generator's latent features. Extensive experiments on two datasets demonstrate that CMA-OT achieves state-of-the-art performance in rhythmic synchronization, perceptual quality, and overall music generation.

</details>


### [279] [A Hybrid LSTM-XGBoost Framework for Multi-Horizon Stock Return Prediction Across Diversified Equity Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13125)
*Seif ElDein Mostafa,Yahia Ahmed,Farah Datwish,Marwa Solayman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种LSTM与XGBoost的两阶段混合架构，用于14只美股、4个预测期限的多期收益率预测；30日测试RMSE为0.0949，但方向准确率的高值主要源于正收益基准率，作者以超出朴素基准的差距作为更可靠信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票收益率预测受非平稳、非线性、低信噪比特性制约，单一模型难以兼顾时序动态建模与特征间非线性拟合能力，且长周期方向准确率易被样本基准率虚高。

Method: 构建两阶段混合模型：LSTM（2层堆叠、64隐藏单元）处理60日滑窗的5维市场序列特征，输出64维时序嵌入；与14个手工技术指标拼接为78维混合特征向量，输入经3折交叉验证网格搜索调参的XGBoost回归器。采用多股票池化训练、严格时序划分与逐股票MinMaxScaling防止前视偏差，在30/90/252/365日四个期限上评估，并提出基于多期限预测的复合投资评分框架。

Result: 30日测试RMSE为0.0949，约为独立LSTM基线的三分之一，在多数股票上与XGBoost-Only基线持平或略优；方向准确率随期限延长而上升，365日达97.6%，但该值主要跟随样本中长周期正收益的高基准率。将方向准确率与"永远预测为正"的朴素基准对比后，短期限上超出基准率的差距被视为更具信息量的信号。

Conclusion: 混合架构在预测误差上显著优于独立LSTM，并接近纯XGBoost基线；长周期方向准确率需结合基准率解读，短周期超额方向准确率更具参考价值，所提复合投资评分框架可为组合排序与决策支持提供辅助。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of equity returns remains a major challenge in computational finance due to the non-stationary, nonlinear, and low signal-to-noise ratio nature of financial time series. This paper proposes a hybrid two-stage architecture that combines a long short-term memory (LSTM) network with an XGBoost gradient-boosted regressor for multi-horizon stock return prediction across a diversified panel of 14 U.S. equities spanning six industry sectors. The LSTM component, comprising two stacked layers with 64 hidden units, processes 60-day sliding windows of five sequential market features to produce 64-dimensional temporal embeddings that encode learned sequential market dynamics. These embeddings are concatenated with 14 hand-crafted technical indicators to form a 78-dimensional hybrid feature vector, which is subsequently passed to an XGBoost regressor tuned via 3-fold cross-validation grid search. The framework is trained on a multi-stock pooled corpus using strict chronological splits and per-stock MinMaxScaling to prevent look-ahead bias, and evaluated across four prediction horizons of 30, 90, 252, and 365 trading days. Experimental results demonstrate that the hybrid model achieves a test RMSE of 0.0949 on the 30-day horizon, roughly one-third that of the standalone LSTM baseline, while marginally matching or surpassing the XGBoost-Only baseline across the majority of stocks. Directional accuracy rises with horizon length, reaching 97.6% at 365 days; we show, however, that this largely tracks the high base rate of positive long-horizon returns in the sample, and we therefore benchmark directional accuracy against a naive always-positive predictor and treat the above-base-rate gap at short horizons as the more informative signal. A composite investment scoring framework derived from multi-horizon predictions is further proposed to support portfolio ranking and decision support.

</details>


### [280] [Rethinking Heterogeneous System Disaggregation for Subquadratic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13134)
*Arya Tschand,Yaosheng Fu,Vikram Sharma Mailthody,Nicolai Oswald,Po-An Tsai,Ritchie Zhao,Oreste Villa,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Karu Sankaralingam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SQD（SubQuadratic Disaggregation），一种按"二次/次二次注意力"而非算子类型划分解码阶段的细粒度异构分离方案，适用于稀疏、线性与滑窗等次二次注意力 LLM，在 DRAM+SRAM 异构系统上显著提升吞吐与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型越来越多采用次二次注意力以降低推理时的显存占用与算力需求，同时保持前沿精度。然而现有服务系统仍以稠密注意力为中心做分离式部署决策，未针对次二次注意力独特的算术强度与内存占用特征进行优化，因而无法充分利用新兴的 DRAM 与纯 SRAM 异构硬件。

Method: SQD 采用细粒度异构分离：对稀疏注意力 LLM，将 decode 拆为需索引完整 KV 的 top-k 选择，以及具有静态内存占用的 top-k 注意力加 FFN；对线性与滑窗注意力 LLM，将 decode 拆为稠密注意力层，以及次二次注意力层加 FFN。方案跨多种次二次注意力变体通用。评估采用调整后的 8×B200 异构系统代理，以及固定功耗预算下 Rubin 加 LPX 系统的解析模型。

Result: 在 8×B200 异构代理上，相比最强纯 GPU 基线，平均 tokens/J 提升：GLM 5.2 为 53%，Nemotron 3 Ultra 为 31%，Gemma 4 31B 为 56%。在 Rubin+LPX 解析模型中，相比最优的注意力-FFN 分离基线，可达延迟收紧 1.2×–1.5×，吞吐最高提升 3.6×。实验还给出面向下一代异构系统服务次二次注意力的芯片与互连配置的架构洞见。

Conclusion: 以"二次/次二次注意力"为切分维度进行解码分离，能更好匹配次二次注意力的算术强度与内存特征，在 DRAM 与纯 SRAM 异构系统上同时取得更高的吞吐与能效；该思路为下一代异构推理系统的芯片与互连设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Frontier language models are more aggressively using subquadratic attention to reduce the memory footprint and compute requirements during inference while still delivering frontier accuracy. While existing systems make dense attention-centric disaggregated serving decisions, we show that disaggregating inference around the unique arithmetic intensity and memory footprint of subquadratic attention LLMs can achieve significant throughput and energy efficiency gains on emerging DRAM-based and SRAM-only heterogeneous systems.
  We introduce SQD (SubQuadratic Disaggregation), a fine-grained heterogeneous disaggregation scheme that splits decode by quadratic and subquadratic attention rather than by operator type, and that applies across subquadratic attention variants. For sparse attention LLMs, we disaggregate decode into top-k selection, which must index through the full KV, and top-k attention plus FFN, which have static memory footprints. For linear and sliding-window attention LLMs, we disaggregate decode into dense attention layers and subquadratic attention layers plus FFN. In an adjusted 8xB200 heterogeneous system proxy, we observe average tokens/J improvements of 53% on GLM 5.2, 31% on Nemotron 3 Ultra, and 56% on Gemma 4 31B over the strongest GPU-only baselines. In an analytical model of a Rubin plus LPX system with fixed power budgets, we observe 1.2x to 1.5x tighter achievable latencies and up to 3.6x higher throughput over the best baseline of attention-FFN disaggregation. Our experiments also reveal architectural insights on chip and interconnect provisioning for next-generation heterogeneous systems serving subquadratic attention.

</details>


### [281] [Evidence-Aligned Local Composition of Discrete Experts for Sequence Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05801)
*Mohammad Panahazari,Usman A. Khan,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"证据对齐局部组合"(evidence-aligned local composition)，通过冻结领域专家的去噪损失估计边际证据，在测试时无区域标签、无路由器的条件下推断逐位置的软专家权重，用于文档恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档可视为来自不同领域文本的离散token序列组合（如README融合散文、代码与配置）。当此类文档被损坏且仅有冻结的领域专家可用时，恢复需要同时判断缺失内容以及每个位置应信任哪个专家，且测试时无区域标签、无可训练路由器。

Method: 将领域专家组合建模为局部组合问题：在给定损坏模型下，由损坏观测的边际证据推断对专家的软性、逐位置加权；证据由专家自身的去噪损失估计，并在位置上进行平滑。权重为软性——真实组合为混合时恢复为混合，单一专家足够时则集中于该专家。

Result: 在类别模拟器、字节级专家以及基于1.3B离散流匹配模型微调的专家上验证：推断权重在自然混合的科学文档上达到0.85的区域字段准确率，在词汇上区域不相交的构造混合上达到0.98。当专家真正不同时，恢复效果优于单一全局权重；当专家趋同时退化为该全局权重，并与专家分离度指标相关。

Conclusion: 证据对齐局部组合方法能在无监督、无路由器条件下有效推断逐位置专家权重，其效果随专家分离度自适应：专家越不同收益越大，专家趋同则退化为全局权重，为冻结专家下的损坏文档恢复提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A document modeled as a discrete sequence of tokens can be thought of as being generated from a composition of texts from different domains; a README file, for example, moves between prose, code, and configuration. When such a document is corrupted and only frozen domain experts are available, restoring it requires deciding both what is missing and which expert to trust at each position, at test time and without region labels or a trained router. We introduce evidence-aligned local composition, which infers a soft, position-wise weighting over the experts from the marginal evidence of the corrupted observation under a given corruption model, estimating the evidence from the experts' own denoising losses and smoothing the weights across positions. Because the weighting is soft, it recovers a mixture when the true composition is mixed and concentrates on one expert when that suffices. Across a categorical simulator, byte-level experts, and experts fine-tuned from a $1.3$B discrete flow-matching model, the inferred weights track the true regions at $0.85$ field accuracy on naturally mixed scientific documents, and at $0.98$ on constructed mixtures whose regions are lexically disjoint. Restoration improves over a single global weight when the experts are genuinely distinct and reduces to it when they converge, tracking a measure of expert separation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [Scenario-Independent Criticality Assessment and Prediction for Vulnerable Road Users in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11947)
*Jörg Gamerdinger,Victor Schwarzenberger,Philipp Schmid,Sven Teufel,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向弱势道路使用者（VRU）的临界性度量，以及场景无关的临界性预测框架；在DeepAccident数据集上，行人临界性分类性能提升最高50%，预测框架超越现有指标275%，F1达0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升安全性是自动驾驶的首要目标，需可靠的安全度量纳入对象类型、速度、临界性等因素。现有临界性度量多针对特定场景，且主要关注车-车交互，缺乏对运动行为更难预测的VRU的专门考虑，也缺乏跨场景通用性。

Method: 提出一种面向VRU、考虑其运动不确定性的新型临界性度量；并引入场景无关的临界性预测框架，适用于所有交通参与者类别，以避免场景专用度量带来的复杂性。使用包含多样安全关键场景的DeepAccident数据集进行评测。

Result: VRU中心临界性度量将行人临界性分类性能提升最高50%；所提临界性预测框架较现有最先进度量提升275%，F1-score达0.96，实现跨全部对象类别的场景无关临界性评估。

Conclusion: 结果表明所提方法在提升自动驾驶系统安全评估的临界性判定方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Increasing safety is the primary objective of automated vehicles. Achieving this goal requires reliable safety metrics that incorporate safety-relevant factors such as object type, velocity, and criticality. A key capability of such metrics is the distinction between critical and non-critical objects, which is addressed through criticality or relevance estimation. Existing criticality metrics are typically designed for specific scenarios and primarily focus on vehicle-to-vehicle interactions. In this paper, we therefore propose a novel criticality metric tailored to vulnerable road users (VRUs), which require special consideration due to their less predictable motion behavior. Furthermore, to avoid the complexity introduced by scenario-specific metrics, we introduce a scenario-independent criticality prediction framework applicable to all traffic participant classes. The effectiveness of both the proposed VRU-centric criticality metric and the criticality prediction framework is evaluated using the DeepAccident dataset, which contains a diverse set of safety-critical traffic scenarios. The proposed VRU-centric criticality metric improves pedestrian criticality classification performance by up to 50 %. In addition, the proposed criticality prediction framework outperforms state-of-the-art metrics by 275 %, achieving an F1-score of 0.96 and enabling scenario-independent criticality assessment across all object classes. These results demonstrate the strong potential of the proposed approaches to enhance criticality assessment for safety evaluation in automated driving systems.

</details>


### [283] [Pelican-Sim 1.0: A General World Model Simulator for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12036)
*Shilong Zou,Shilin Zhang,Yingji Zhang,Yuhang Huang,Yi Zhang,Zeyuan Ding,Han Dong,Junwei Liao,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Pelican-Sim 1.0，一个面向具身智能的通用世界模型模拟器，可从视觉上下文与机器人动作预测未来观测。其四大设计——统一动作表示、动作-视觉注入、稀疏 MoE、高效 rollout 生成——在约百万条真实与仿真轨迹上训练后，显著提升动作可控性与视频质量，并在 RoboTwin 上完成四项下游应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能需要能预测未来观测的世界模型以支持下游学习与决策，但现有方法难以在异构本体、场景与任务间保持统一有效性与可控性，且生成效率不足。

Method: 提出 Pelican-Sim 1.0，包含四项设计：(1) 28 维统一动作值空间覆盖主流本体；(2) 基于 URDF 与相机渲染的动作视频实现动作-视觉注入，桥接动作与像素；(3) 稀疏混合专家层增强异构动力学容量并吸收动作模态，减少模态间冲突；(4) 通过因果适配与少步蒸馏得到四步自回归模拟器。在约百万条真实与仿真轨迹上训练。

Result: PSNR 相比最强基线在 AgiBotWorld Beta 提升 4.636、RoboMIND 提升 2.080、RoboTwin 提升 10.343；适配 EWMBench DYN 分数在 RoboTwin 提升 0.426；动作-视觉注入较替代融合基线 PSNR +0.904，稀疏 MoE 较稠密骨干 FVD -6.530；四步模型较 35 步模型加速 5.67 倍。下游任务：每任务 50 条演示加入 500 条生成轨迹使策略成功率由 70% 升至 93%；策略评估在五个检查点上 Pearson 相关达 0.994；动作选择相对成功率增益 47.7%，策略改进增益 20.3%。

Conclusion: Pelican-Sim 1.0 在动作可控性与视频质量上大幅优于现有基线，并在策略增强、评估与改进等下游应用中有效；跨轨迹、场景、物体、本体与视角的定性泛化表明其具备通用世界模型模拟器的潜力。

Abstract: In this technical report, we propose Pelican-Sim 1.0, a general world model simulator for embodied intelligence that predicts future observations from visual context and robot actions to support downstream learning and decision making. The model incorporates four key design features: (1) Unified action representation: a 28-dimensional action value space covering most mainstream embodiments, keeping one model valid across heterogeneous devices. (2) Action-visual injection: URDF- and camera-rendered action videos bridge actions and pixels, giving markedly better controllability across embodiments, scenes, and tasks (PSNR +0.904 over alternative fusion baselines). (3) Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE): sparse MoE layers add capacity for heterogeneous dynamics and absorb the action modality while reducing inter-modality conflict (FVD -6.530 vs. the dense backbone). (4) Efficient rollout generation: causal adaptation and few-step distillation yield a four-step autoregressive simulator, achieving a 5.67-fold speedup over the 35-step model. Benefiting from these designs, we train on approximately one million real-world and simulated trajectories and obtain large gains in action controllability and video quality: PSNR improves over the strongest evaluated baselines by 4.636 on AgiBotWorld Beta, 2.080 on RoboMIND, and 10.343 on RoboTwin, with the adapted EWMBench DYN score up 0.426 on RoboTwin. Relying on this, four downstream applications on RoboTwin succeed: 500 generated trajectories added to 50 demonstrations per task raise policy success from 70% to 93%; policy evaluation reaches a Pearson correlation of 0.994 across five checkpoints; and relative success gains reach 47.7% for action selection and 20.3% for policy improvement. Qualitative generalization across trajectory, scene, object, embodiment, and viewpoint shifts highlights its potential as a general-purpose world model simulator.

</details>


### [284] [Multi-Objective Agent-Based Model Predictive Controller for Plug-and-Play Vehicle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12108)
*Jiaming Zhong,Ladan Khoshnevisan,Shucheng Huang,Mohammad Pirani,Yash Vardhan Pant,Amir Khajepour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多目标AMPC分布式控制方案，将ADMM推广为通用控制策略，在解耦目标的同时逼近全局优化，并在实车验证中取得与集成MPC近似的最优性且更具灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆控制功能集成趋势下，需要"即插即用"的灵活可靠控制系统，但传统集成式MPC难以满足该需求；已有的基于智能体的MPC（AMPC）虽为分布式协同框架，却难以处理智能体间耦合或相互依赖时的分布式冲突目标。

Method: 提出多目标AMPC的分布式控制方案，将交替方向乘子法（ADMM）适配为通用控制策略，在解耦目标的同时逼近全局优化；系统性地构建三种保持收敛性的公式化表达，分别处理控制正则化与不等式约束，并首次应用于复杂车辆控制系统。

Result: 在两个多目标拓扑的车辆控制场景中进行测试，通过仿真对比不同公式化表达，并将计算效率最高的方案部署于电动汽车开展真实世界评估。结果表明，该多目标AMPC能近似收敛至与集成MPC相同的全局最优，同时具备更高灵活性并有望降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 多目标AMPC为车辆功能集成提供了一种兼顾灵活性、可靠性与计算效率的分布式控制路径，可作为传统集成式MPC的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Functional integration is a growing trend in vehicle control, often involving the coordination of multiple controllers to achieve various objectives simultaneously. The need for flexibility and reliability has led to a "plug-and-play" approach in control system design, which presents challenges for traditional integrated model predictive control (MPC). Agent-based model predictive control (AMPC) has recently emerged as a distributed solution that treats controllers as agents, creating a collaborative framework among them to reach a common goal. However, this approach struggles to manage distributed conflicting objectives when agents are coupled or interdependent. To address this, we propose a novel, practical distributed control scheme called multi-objective AMPC, which adapts the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) into a general control strategy that approximates global optimization while decoupling objectives. We systematically develop three formulations that maintain convergence while addressing control regularization and inequality constraints, applying them to complex vehicle control systems for the first time. The proposed method has been tested on two vehicle control scenarios with a multi-objective topology. Different formulations are compared through simulations, and the most computationally efficient one was implemented on an electric vehicle for real-world evaluations. The results demonstrate that the proposed multi-objective AMPC can converge approximately to the same global optimum as integrated MPC with greater flexibility and the potential to reduce computational costs.

</details>


### [285] [A Data-Driven Distributed Control Scheme: Learning Multi-Objective Agent-Based MPC for Path-Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12142)
*Jiaming Zhong,Reza Valiollahi Mehrizi,Yash Vardhan Pant,Amir Khajepour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习型多目标智能体模型预测控制（AMPC）框架，基于ADMM解耦多控制器系统，并通过学习式迭代初始化与数据管理提升实时性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成式MPC在处理多智能体、多目标耦合问题时面临计算负担重、维护困难等实际挑战，需兼顾设计灵活性与计算效率。

Method: 在信息交换假设下，将AMPC设计为ADMM定制形式以解耦系统并迭代逼近集成方案性能；提出基于学习的迭代初始化加速收敛，并设计数据管理方法与认证模块保障实时性与学习可靠性。

Result: 在自动驾驶车辆组合路径跟踪仿真中，所提方案控制性能与集成方案一致，计算时间减少43.5%；引入学习后较无学习方案再节省88.6%计算时间。

Conclusion: 该学习型多目标AMPC在保持与集成MPC同等控制性能的同时显著降低计算开销，适合实时实现。

Abstract: Agent-based model predictive control (AMPC) has recently been proposed for vehicle systems with various controllers, such as differential braking and torque vectoring, where controllers are regarded as distributed agents contributing to the same objective. However, this scheme is challenging in handling multiple conflicting objectives with coupled agents. A common approach for such tasks is the integrated MPC, where all objectives and agents are stacked together in one optimization. Nevertheless, as more agents and objectives are involved, the integrated MPC will face challenges like computational burdens and maintenance difficulties in practice. To this end, this paper proposes a learning multi-objective AMPC that can improve design flexibility and computing efficiency. First, under the assumption of information exchange, a multi-objective AMPC tailored from the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) is proposed to decouple the system and achieve the same performance as the integrated scheme iteratively. Second, a learning-based method for initializing iterations is proposed to accelerate convergence. In addition, a data management method is proposed for real-time efficiency, and an authentication module is designed for learning reliability. We compare the proposed scheme against the integrated scheme via a combined path-tracking simulation for autonomous vehicles with various controllers. The proposed scheme achieves the same control performance as the integrated one while reducing the computational time by 43.5%. Furthermore, the learning-based method saves 88.6% more computational time than without learning, making it suitable for real-time implementation.

</details>


### [286] [Battery-Aware Predictive Trajectory Planning and Control for Multirotors Under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12188)
*Krishna Bhavithavya Kidambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多旋翼的电池感知预测轨迹规划与控制框架，在存在空间局部扰动的条件下通过闭环车辆-电机-电池传播评估候选轨迹，权衡跟踪精度、能量消耗与执行器能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼在空间局部扰动下飞行时，控制需求会显著增加，进而影响电能消耗、电池状态演化及终端电压相关的执行器能力。传统轨迹规划通常忽略这些闭环能量与电池动态耦合，导致所选轨迹在实际执行中能量效率与跟踪性能不佳。

Method: 构建电池感知的预测轨迹规划与控制框架：候选轨迹通过闭环“车辆-电机-电池”传播进行评估，使扰动引起的控制需求、电能、电池演化及终端电压依赖的执行器能力进入规划过程。采用降阶电池模型，并与独立实现的Simscape等效电路参考进行数值基准对比；通过规划器消融实验及多反馈控制器执行实验验证。

Result: 降阶电池模型相对Simscape参考的功率NRMSE为0.64%，累计能量偏差低于0.7%。在150 s、640 m、含三个扰动区域的飞行任务中，所选轨迹相对扰动感知固定参考基线降低电能消耗7.46%，位置跟踪RMSE降低约72%。规划器消融表明电池相关项在标称SOC下不起约束作用，但在电池耗尽压力条件下会改变所选轨迹。多控制器执行实验显示控制器选择会改变跟踪精度、能量消耗与执行器利用率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 在轨迹选择阶段考虑预测的闭环能量与电池-执行器后果具有明显收益，可同时改善能量效率与跟踪性能，并揭示控制器选择对多目标权衡的影响。

Abstract: This paper presents a battery-aware predictive trajectory-planning and control framework for multirotors operating under spatially localized disturbances. Candidate trajectories are evaluated through closed-loop vehicle--motor--battery propagation, allowing disturbance-induced control demand, electrical energy, battery evolution, and terminal-voltage-dependent actuator capability to enter the planning process. % A reduced-order battery model is numerically benchmarked against an independently implemented Simscape equivalent-circuit reference, with a power NRMSE of $0.64\%$ and a cumulative-energy discrepancy below $0.7\%$. % In a $150$-s, $640$-m mission containing three disturbance regions, the selected trajectory reduces electrical energy consumption by $7.46\%$ and position-tracking RMSE by approximately $72\%$ relative to the disturbance-aware fixed-reference baseline. % Planner ablations show that battery-dependent terms are nonbinding at nominal SOC but alter the selected trajectory under a depleted-battery stress condition. % Execution with multiple feedback controllers further demonstrates that controller selection changes the tradeoff among tracking accuracy, energy consumption, and actuator utilization. % The results demonstrate the benefit of accounting for predicted closed-loop energetic and battery--actuator consequences during trajectory selection.

</details>


### [287] [Guardrailed Meta-Agent Loops: Stress-Testing Policy Pinning, Budget Bounds, and Crash Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12216)
*Qinzhen Ma,Jialin Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GuardrailLoop，一个基于仿真的测试床，使三条运行契约（策略保持、计算记账、崩溃恢复）可联合测试，并通过50种子配对实验与240次崩溃注入验证其可行性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进的智能体工作流存在审计难题：同一控制器既能改变自身行为，又能改变判定该行为的条件。因此需要将人类定义策略的保持、计算记账与崩溃恢复等契约变成可执行、可检验的边界。

Method: 设计哈希固定的策略（固定目标、范围、评估身份、预算与发布条件），将机器主导的演化限制在代码自有的特征目录与有界旋钮内；构建可执行边界与评估协议，把有用适应、状态恢复与重复执行区分开来。开展50种子2×2配对实验，并枚举240次崩溃注入，配合资源漂移、急停开关、完整性与输出防护矩阵检查。

Result: 轮阶段增长使目标达成度提高+1.00，达成目标的受限平均计算量减少56.97模拟GPU小时（95%配对自助区间[-58.91,-54.70]）；空闲增长无测量的效用影响。240次崩溃注入中全部恢复既定结果，但仅210次保持规范化轨迹：30次提交前崩溃重复了一次规划器调用。各防护矩阵均通过指定检查。

Conclusion: 成功的结果恢复不足以证明恰好一次执行。本文仅在一个已校准的确定性测试床内确立一致性，而非一般安全性或真实世界的自我改进能力。

Abstract: Self-improving agent workflows create an audit problem when the same controller can change both its behavior and the conditions under which that behavior is judged. We present GuardrailLoop, a simulation-based testbed that makes three operational contracts jointly testable: preservation of human-defined policy, compute accounting at every recorded execution prefix, and recovery of a specified scientific state after crashes. A hash-pinned policy fixes goals, scope, evaluation identity, budget, and release conditions; machine-directed evolution is restricted to a code-owned feature catalog and bounded knobs. The contribution is an executable boundary and an evaluation protocol that separates useful adaptation, state recovery, and repeated execution. In a paired 50-seed 2 x 2 study, round-stage growth changes target attainment by +1.00 and restricted mean compute to target by -56.97 simulated GPU-hours (95% paired-bootstrap interval [-58.91,-54.70]); idle growth has zero measured utility effect. Across 240 enumerated crash injections, all runs recover the defined outcome, but only 210 preserve the normalized trace: 30 pre-commit crashes repeat a planner call. Resource-drift, kill-switch, integrity, and output-guard matrices satisfy their specified checks. These findings show why successful outcome recovery is insufficient evidence of exactly-once execution. They establish conformance within one calibrated deterministic testbed, rather than general safety or real-world self-improvement.

</details>


### [288] [Chain-SLAM: Globally Consistent Backend for Multi-Session LiDAR SLAM via Chained Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12221)
*Zhiheng Li,Xinhao Liu,Juexiao Zhang,Yongqing Liang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chain-SLAM —— 一个面向大规模多会话 LiDAR SLAM 的在线后端，通过链式回环传播跨会话几何约束，实现全局一致的多会话地图对齐与复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 LiDAR SLAM 在长时空跨度下难以保持一致性，尤其是当需要对跨多个会话采集的地图进行融合时，跨会话（inter-session）位姿对齐与地图复用缺乏稳定、可扩展的一致性保障。

Method: 1) 链式回环机制：通过邻接图（adjacency graph）在跨会话关键帧之间高效传播几何约束，由可靠的短时域回环触发长时域一致性；2) 以 GNSS 邻近性（GNSS-proximity）地点识别初始化跨会话语义对齐；3) 在新旧轨迹与已加载地图统一因子图（factor graph）中实现在线回环检测与联合优化，同时维护会话内与会话间几何一致性；4) 无需动态物体移除，跨平台鲁棒且超参数调节需求低。

Result: 实验表明轨迹精度得到提升，并在大规模数据集上实现鲁棒的多会话地图集成。作者开源代码以支持大规模多会话 LiDAR SLAM 的可复现研究。

Conclusion: Chain-SLAM 为大尺度多会话 LiDAR SLAM 提供了一种可持续在线运行的后端方案，通过链式回环与统一因子图优化，在无需动态物体去除与大量调参的条件下实现了跨会话全局一致性，并已开源（项目站点：https://ai4ce.github.io/Chain-SLAM/）。

Abstract: Maintaining consistency over long spatial and temporal horizons remains a fundamental challenge in large-scale LiDAR SLAM, particularly when integrating maps collected across multiple sessions. We present Chain-SLAM, a LiDAR SLAM backend enabling online multi-session map alignment and reuse with global consistency at large scale. We implement a chained loop closure mechanism that efficiently propagates geometric constraints across inter-session keyframes through an adjacency graph, enabling robust long-horizon consistency triggered by reliable short-horizon loop closures. The system initializes inter-session alignment with GNSS-proximity place recognition, then performs on-the-fly loop closure detections and joint optimization of loaded maps and newly acquired trajectories within a unified factor graph, maintaining both inter- and intra-session geometric consistency without dynamic object removal, and cross-platform robustness with minimal hyperparameter tuning. Experimental results show improved trajectory accuracy and robust multi-session integration on large-scale datasets. We release our source code to support reproducible research in large-scale multi-session LiDAR SLAM. Project site: https://ai4ce.github.io/Chain-SLAM/

</details>


### [289] [DIA: Denoising Intermediate Advantage for Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12245)
*Arjun Sohal,Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Alan Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Denoising Intermediate Advantage (DIA)，一种在扩散策略微调中为每个去噪步骤分配去噪级优势的策略梯度方法，在多个机器人操作基准上稳定超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯行为克隆训练的扩散机器人策略受限于演示数据的质量与覆盖范围，需借助强化学习通过交互进一步提升。现有将扩散策略微调建模为外层环境MDP与内层去噪MDP的策略梯度方法，对构建一个动作块的所有去噪步骤分配相同的环境级信用，无法区分哪些中间去噪决策对最终回报贡献最大，导致信用分配粗糙、优化效率受限。

Method: DIA 在部分去噪的动作上学习价值函数，据此为生成过程的每一步构造去噪级优势（denoising-level advantage）；再将该内层信用信号与标准的环境级 PPO 优势相结合，从而在整个去噪链上提供状态相关的信用分配。

Result: 在 Robomimic、FurnitureBench、Franka Kitchen 和 D3IL 四个基准上，DIA 的最终性能持续优于现有扩散策略微调方法；此外它能更高效地到达成功状态，并能更大程度地偏离预训练行为分布，从而发现基线方法无法企及的任务级策略与子任务序列。

Conclusion: 通过将信用分配细化到去噪步骤层面并与环境级优势融合，DIA 改进了扩散策略的强化学习微调，兼具更高回报、更高样本效率与更强的探索能力，表明精细化的中间信用分配是提升生成式策略微调的关键。

Abstract: Diffusion-based robot policies have become widely used in robotic manipulation, where they are typically trained with behavior cloning. However, policies trained purely from demonstrations are limited by the quality and coverage of the available data. Reinforcement learning can further improve the performance of these pretrained policies through interaction. A common approach is to use policy-gradient methods that formulate diffusion-policy fine-tuning as an outer environment MDP together with an inner denoising MDP. However, existing methods typically assign the same environment-level credit to all denoising steps used to construct an action chunk, without distinguishing which intermediate decisions contributed most to the final return. We introduce Denoising Intermediate Advantage (DIA), a policy-gradient method that learns a value function over partially denoised actions and uses it to construct a denoising- level advantage for each step of the generative process. DIA com- bines this inner credit signal with the standard environment-level PPO advantage, providing state-dependent credit throughout the denoising chain. Across Robomimic, FurnitureBench, Franka Kitchen, and D3IL, DIA consistently improves final performance over existing diffusion-policy fine-tuning methods. Beyond final reward, DIA reaches successful states more efficiently and can shift farther from the pretrained behavior distribution, enabling it to discover more effective and efficient task-level strategies and subtask sequences that baseline methods fail to reach.

</details>


### [290] [Trajectory Bundle Method in SE(3) for Black-Box Fixed-Wing Aircraft Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12248)
*Matthew D. Osburn,Cameron K. Peterson,John L. Salmon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹束方法（TBM）在SE(3)上隐式求解刚体系统轨迹优化，实现无导数、非欧几里得的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体系统动态可行的轨迹优化自然定义于SE(3)，但当动力学仅以无导数黑箱形式给出时极具挑战性。

Method: 在Lie代数中构造束，并通过指数/对数映射在非线性刚体动力学中传播；证明欧氏TBM插值误差受束直径二次约束，并推广至SE(3)，其界还依赖Log映射的局部Lipschitz常数。

Result: 数值实验验证了误差界；在无显式动力学、气动或碰撞模型及导数的情况下，完成穿越旋转开口的固定翼特技无碰撞机动优化。

Conclusion: SE(3) TBM可在仅黑箱动力学下实现无导数的非欧几里得轨迹优化，适用于复杂特技机动规划。

Abstract: Dynamically feasible trajectory optimization for rigid-body systems is naturally formulated on the special Euclidean group SE(3) but is challenging when dynamics are available only as black-box computations without derivatives. This paper formulates the Trajectory Bundle Method (TBM) for motion planning implicitly on SE(3). Bundles are constructed in the Lie algebra and propagated through nonlinear rigid-body dynamics using exponential and logarithmic maps, enabling derivative-free planning of non-Euclidean trajectories. We show that Euclidean TBM interpolation error is bounded quadratically by bundle diameter and extend this result to SE(3), where the bound additionally depends on a local Lipschitz constant of the Log map. Numerical experiments corroborate these bounds. Finally, we demonstrate SE(3) TBM by optimizing an acrobatic, collision-free fixed-wing maneuver through a rotated aperture without explicit models or derivatives of the vehicle dynamics, aerodynamics, or collision model.

</details>


### [291] [Pneumatic neurons for soft robots enable inflate-and-fire networks for rhythmic motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12258)
*Dongting Li,Michael Tolley,Nick Gravish*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Pneu-ron——一种受漏电积分放电(leaky integrate-and-fire)模型启发的软体机器人控制架构,将能量转换、逻辑与驱动统一于单个软体模块中,实现了无需集中式电子控制器的自适应软体机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物通过分布式神经环路协调运动,而软体机器人通常仍依赖外部集中式电子器件进行控制。如何在摆脱集中式电子控制器的同时保持对环境的响应能力,是软体机器人领域的前沿挑战。

Method: 提出Pneu-ron软体驱动器:每个模块集成低沸点流体(LBF)、加热器与机械开关,构成可自激单元。通过加热使LBF沸腾膨胀,触发相邻模块的兴奋与抑制,称为"膨胀-放电"(inflate-and-fire)过程。将多个Pneu-ron互连为兴奋-抑制环,产生稳定的顺序振荡;并对网络进行动力学建模。

Result: 互联为兴奋-抑制环的Pneu-ron网络可产生稳定顺序振荡,其频率由材料动力学与环境条件涌现决定;利用Pneu-ron的膨胀实现驱动,可推动软体机器人的振荡式运动;网络在机械负载与热扰动下仍能维持振荡,通过材料物理而非计算实现自适应。动力学建模揭示了一个无量纲分岔图,决定网络的振荡行为。

Conclusion: 将逻辑与驱动编码到材料级模块中,为构建自适应、无需电子控制器的软体机器人提供了新途径。

Abstract: Animals coordinate their movements through distributed neural circuits, but soft robots still typically depend on external, centralized electronics for control. Building soft robots that operate without centralized electronic controllers while remaining responsive to their environment remains a frontier challenge in soft robotics. In this work we introduce a soft-robot control architecture inspired by leaky integrate-and-fire models of biological neural circuits. The Pneumatic neuron (Pneu-ron) is a soft actuator that unifies energy conversion, logic, and actuation in one component. Each module combines a low-boiling-point fluid (LBF), a heater, and a mechanical switch into a self-excitable unit. Boiling the LBF inflates the module and triggers excitation and inhibition of adjacent modules in a process we call "inflate-and-fire". When interconnected into excitatory-inhibitory rings, Pneu-rons generate stable, sequential oscillations whose frequency emerges from the material dynamics and environmental conditions. By harnessing the inflation of Pneu-rons for actuation these networks can drive oscillatory locomotion of soft robots. Pneu-ron networks sustain oscillation under mechanical load and thermal variations, adapting through material physics rather than computation. Dynamical modeling of these networks reveals a dimensionless bifurcation diagram that dictates the network's oscillatory behavior. Encoding logic and actuation into material-level modules presents a new avenue for adaptive, electronics controller-free, soft robots.

</details>


### [292] [AnchorVLN: Geometry-Anchored Vision-Language Grounding Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12285)
*Long Giang Vu,Chengkai Yao,Yuxin Liu,FNU Aryan,Rajath Chandrashekar Aralikatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorVLN/EMBODIED-NAV-MCP：VLM 负责语义，几何负责度量，通过 MCP 工具约束实现开放词汇室内视觉语言导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内开放词汇 VLN 需要智能体遵循自然语言指令、定位物体并回答空间问题，且无预建地图与固定物体词表。多模态 VLM 具备开放词汇 grounding 与零样本推理能力，但难以直接从图像输出可靠的度量量（距离、方位、比较性空间关系）。现有方案依赖手工几何流水线或将输出改为路点，需针对不同机器人、任务或词表修改控制栈，通用性差。

Method: 提出 AnchorVLN，核心规则为“VLM 提议语义，几何决定度量”。实现为 EMBODIED-NAV-MCP：由 VLM 智能体通过一小组可调用工具驱动的 MCP 服务器；工具 schema 不接受以米为单位的距离或以弧度为单位的方位，从而在不修改下游自主栈的前提下强制语义—几何边界。

Result: 在 CMU VLN Challenge 2026 两项任务评测：15 个场景 30 道指令跟随题，以及冻结的 45 题物体指代集。完整系统指令跟随准确率 64.4%，移除 controller modeling 后下降 13.3 个百分点（t=2.77）。物体指代任务中，几何锚定在 45 题中有 10 题超过挑战重叠阈值，而直接坐标估计为 0/45；中心误差中位数从 3.37 m 降至 2.48 m。

Conclusion: 通过让 VLM 只负责语义、几何负责度量，并以 MCP 工具 schema 施加约束，可在不改动下游控制栈的情况下实现开放词汇室内 VLN，在指令跟随与物体指代两项任务上均取得有效提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) in unseen indoor environments is useful in real-world robotics, where an agent must follow natural-language instructions, locate objects, and answer spatial questions without a pre-built map or fixed object vocabulary. Multimodal vision-language models (VLMs) provide strong open-vocabulary grounding and zero-shot reasoning, but struggle to emit reliable metric quantities such as range, bearing, and comparative spatial relations directly from images. Existing approaches address this by folding geometry into hand-engineered pipelines or asking models to output waypoints, requiring changes to the control stack for different robots, tasks, or vocabularies. We introduce AnchorVLN, an open-vocabulary VLN system built on a simple rule: the VLM proposes semantics; geometry decides metrics. It is realised as EMBODIED-NAV-MCP, a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server driven by a VLM agent through a compact set of callable tools. Since no tool accepts distance in metres or bearing in radians, the schema enforces the semantic-geometry boundary without modifying the downstream autonomy stack. We benchmark both tasks of the CMU Vision-Language Navigation Challenge 2026: 30 instruction-following questions over 15 scenes and a frozen 45-question object-reference set. The full system achieves 64.4 percent on instruction following, dropping by 13.3 percentage points without controller modeling (t = 2.77). On object reference, geometric anchoring clears the challenge overlap threshold on 10 of 45 questions, versus 0 of 45 for direct coordinate estimation, reducing median center error from 3.37 m to 2.48 m.

</details>


### [293] [Mission Performance: Automatic and Adaptive Race Pace Progression for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12292)
*Giovanni Lambertini,Matteo Pini,Nicola Musiu,Ayoub Raji,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向全自动驾驶赛车的Mission Performance模块，通过对性能目标的自适应管理（而非修改车辆模型参数）来加速圈速提升并保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时抓地力估计难以安全可靠地应用于运动规划与控制等关键模块，因此需要一种不依赖抓地力估计、又能逐步逼近极限性能的管理方案。

Method: 该模块由预定义的渐进流程组成，在运行初期进行轮胎升温；随后按分扇区（per-sector）持续监测安全性与车辆动力学指标，自适应地降低、维持或提升各扇区的性能水平，使其逐步收敛至允许的最大值；通过调整规划与控制模块的目标性能而非改变车辆模型参数来实现引导。

Result: 该方案在全自动驾驶的Dallara Superformula赛车EAV-25上进行验证，测试场地为Yas Marina赛道，参与Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League (A2RL) 第二赛季。

Conclusion: 该模块能够在不依赖实时抓地力估计的前提下，安全地加速圈速优化进程，实现纵向、横向及复合性能的自动管理，并在实际赛事环境中验证了有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe the Mission Performance module implemented for a fully autonomous racing car to automatically manage the longitudinal, lateral, and combined performances, aiming to speedup the laptime progression while assuring safety. Motivated by the difficulty and risks of applying the real-time estimation of the grip to critical modules like the motion planner and controller, the Mission Performance guides these modules adapting their target performance instead of changing the vehicle model parameters. The module is formed by pre-defined progressions to warm up the tires at the beginning of a run. Then, the system continuously monitors safety and vehicle dynamics metrics on a per-sector basis to adaptively reduce, maintain, or increase the performance levels for each sector, progressively converging toward the maximum allowed value. The solution's effectiveness is demonstrated on the EAV-25, a fully autonomous Dallara Superformula, at the Yas Marina Circuit during the Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League (A2RL) Season 2.

</details>


### [294] [DATAFARM: Distribution-Aligned Task and Motion Planning for Fine-Tuning Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12316)
*Samrat Sahoo,Yixuan Huang,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DATAFARM，将预训练分布引入 TAMP 轨迹生成，使生成数据在机器人关节配置、运动风格和时间执行特征上与预训练数据对齐，从而显著提升 VLA 微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采集高质量机器人数据是训练机器人基础模型的核心难题。TAMP 虽可规模化生成演示数据，但实验发现直接用原始 TAMP 轨迹微调预训练 VLA 模型收益甚微，作者推测原因在于规划器生成轨迹与 VLA 预训练数据之间存在行为分布不匹配。

Method: 提出 DATAFARM（Distribution-Aligned Task And motion planning for Fine-tuning A Robot foundation Model），在 TAMP 轨迹生成过程中直接引入预训练分布约束，从机器人关节配置、运动风格和时间执行曲线三个维度将生成轨迹与预训练数据对齐。

Result: 在三个 TAMP 可完成的桌面操作任务及一个超出 TAMP 能力的叠布任务上评估：DATAFARM 平均成功率达 56.7%，远高于原始 TAMP 的 8.3%，接近人类遥操作的 61.7%。在微调分布外的可变形物体操作任务上，微调模型保持 85% 成功率，预训练模型为 90%。

Conclusion: 将规划器生成的演示数据与预训练分布对齐，可使 TAMP 成为 VLA 微调的有效数据来源，同时较好保持模型的原有泛化能力。

Abstract: Collecting high-quality robot data remains a fundamental challenge for training robot foundation models. Task and motion planning (TAMP) offers a scalable way to generate demonstrations, but our experiments show that raw TAMP trajectories provide surprisingly little benefit when used to fine-tune pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models, despite successfully solving the target tasks. We hypothesize that this failure arises from a behavioral distribution mismatch between planner-generated trajectories and the data used to pretrain the VLA. To address this mismatch, we introduce DATAFARM: Distribution-Aligned Task And motion planning for Fine-tuning A Robot foundation Model, an approach that incorporates the pretraining distribution directly into TAMP trajectory generation. DATAFARM aligns generated trajectories with the pretraining data in robot joint configurations, motion style, and temporal execution profiles. We evaluate DATAFARM on three tabletop manipulation tasks that TAMP can perform and a cloth-folding task beyond the capability of TAMP. DATAFARM achieves an average success rate of 56.7%, substantially outperforming raw TAMP (8.3%) while approaching human teleoperation (61.7%). On Deformable Object Manipulation, which is outside the fine-tuning distribution, the fine-tuned model retains 85% success, compared with 90% for the pretrained model. These results show that aligning planner-generated demonstrations with the pretraining distribution can make TAMP an effective source of data for VLA fine-tuning. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/datafarm/

</details>


### [295] [DWMP: Leveraging Dual World Models for Humanoid Obstacle Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12347)
*Rongjun Jin,Jianming Ma,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DWMP框架，为类人机器人障碍穿越提供本体感知与视觉的双世界模型表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人穿越杂乱障碍场时，需依赖本体感知与第一人称视觉观测。现有方法处理多模态观测时未区分其特性：本体感知维度低但受高度非线性动力学支配，自我中心视觉维度高噪声大且冗余。论文旨在针对这些差异设计互补表征以提升学习效果。

Method: DWMP包含两个世界模型：1）基于Koopman的动力学世界模型，将本体感知观测提升到潜在空间，使其时间演化近似线性，便于actor学习动力学特征；2）基于RSSM的视觉世界模型，将自我中心深度观测压缩为紧凑的随机隐状态，同时保留障碍物相关几何信息。学生策略接收二者融合的潜在表征生成动作。

Result: 仿真实验及在Unitree G1类人机器人上的实验表明，DWMP在障碍穿越性能上优于基线方法，并支持在随机化障碍布局下的真实世界部署。

Conclusion: 通过为不同模态设计特性匹配的世界模型表征（线性化本体动力学+压缩视觉感知），可有效提升类人机器人障碍穿越的性能与实机泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots must traverse cluttered obstacle fields using onboard proprioceptive and visual observations, yet existing methods usually process multimodal observations without explicitly considering their different characteristics: proprioceptive observations are low-dimensional but governed by highly nonlinear robot dynamics, while egocentric visual observations are high-dimensional, noisy, and redundant. We propose DWMP (Dual World Model Policy), a framework that provides the actor with separate but complementary world-model representations for humanoid obstacle traversal. A Koopman-based dynamics world model lifts proprioceptive observations into a latent space where their temporal evolution is approximately linear, making the dynamics features easier for the actor to learn from. An RSSM-based visual world model compresses egocentric depth observations into compact stochastic states while preserving obstacle-related geometry. The student policy receives the fused latent representation for action generation, combining linearized proprioceptive dynamics with compressed visual perception. Experiments in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot show that DWMP improves obstacle traversal performance over baselines and supports real-world deployment under randomized obstacle layouts.

</details>


### [296] [A Deployable Architecture for Robot-Mediated Tasks (DART): Evaluation in Socially Assistive Robot-Guided Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Exercises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12349)
*Mina Kian,Lydia Ignatova,Jiong Wang,Ji Min Lee,Jiancheng Li,Qianwei Guo,Emily Weiss,Amy O'Connell,Kaitlin Zareno,Jiani Li,Reyna Patel,Leyaa George,Minyu Huang,Justin Yang,Maja J. Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DART，一种通过Web应用与云基础设施扩展低成本社交辅助机器人（SAR）能力的可部署架构，并在帮助焦虑大学生完成CBT作业的HRI系统中验证其有效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交辅助机器人可支持结构化的健康与福祉干预，但硬件与成本限制制约了交互复杂度及长期真实场景部署，因此需要一种可扩展、低成本且易于部署的架构来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 提出DART架构，整合Web应用与云端基础设施，实现可视化内容、用户输入、远程计算与持久化数据存储，并与机器人的物理形态、语音和动作协同。以低成本开源Blossom机器人为平台，构建面向高焦虑大学生的CBT家庭作业全栈HRI系统，经参与式设计、多轮用户研究迭代，随后开展103人实验室研究和4人六周居家部署评估。

Result: 实验室研究中，参与者单次会话内压力、状态焦虑与负性情绪显著下降，系统可用性量表（SUS）均分78.89；居家部署中SUS均分达87.5，质性反馈正面。两组参与者均将语音输入、视觉呈现及Web-机器人同步列为优先改进方向。

Conclusion: 结果表明DART是有效架构，可在单次实验室会话与真实长期部署两种场景下扩展低成本SAR的能力。

Abstract: Socially assistive robots (SARs) can support structured health and well-being interventions, but hardware and cost constraints limit interaction complexity and longitudinal real-world deployments. We present DART: Deployable Architecture for Robot-Mediated Tasks, an architecture that extends SARs through a web application and cloud infrastructure, enabling visual content, user input, remote computation, and persistent data storage synergistically with the robot's physical embodiment, speech, and movement. We evaluated DART by instantiating it in an interatively-developed full-stack HRI system for helping university students with elevated generalized anxiety to complete cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT) homework exercises. The resulting system, which used the low-cost open-source Blossom robot platform, was refined and evaluated through a participatory design process and multiple user studies, and finally evaluated in an in-lab study with 103 participants, and then a six-week in-home deployment with four participants. In the in-lab evaluation, participants showed significant within-session reductions in stress, state anxiety, and negative affect, and gave the platform a mean System Usability Scale score of 78.89. In the home deployment, the mean System Usability Scale score was 87.5, with positive qualitative feedback on usability. Participants across both groups identified speech input, visual presentation, and web-robot synchronization as priorities for improvement. These findings validate DART as an effective architecture for extending the capabilities of a low-cost SAR in both in-lab single-session and in real-world longitudinal deployments.

</details>


### [297] [READ: Learning Risk-Informed Fields for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12371)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Yuanxin Tian,Zehong Ke,Jinhao Li,Hao Cheng,Zhenhua Xu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 READ 框架，从互补的几何与行为约束中学习显式、与规划对齐的风险表征，并将其实例化为可微的连续时空场，用于候选轨迹的低风险对齐与评估，在 NAVSIM 上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的规划器虽能通过潜在场景特征与轨迹解码器捕捉环境对机动的影响，但环境因素与候选动作之间的关系往往是隐式的，难以检查、诊断或改进场景上下文对轨迹安全性的影响；经典安全场虽提供显式空间表征，但其风险形状与相对权重预先设定，无法随场景自适应。

Method: READ 从互补的几何约束与行为约束中学习显式、规划对齐的风险表征，并将其实例化为连续时空场，从而支持沿候选轨迹的可微查询；通过学习到的场将场景理解与动作选择相连接，鼓励预测轨迹对齐低风险区域，同时保留用于轨迹评估与细化的可微接口；该框架可与端到端规划器及视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型集成。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上，READ 在匹配的端到端骨干网络上取得一致增益，并在 VLA 设定下表现强劲；在 NAVSIM v2 上也取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了学习到的空间风险作为一种显式、可自适应的安全规划表征的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires more than recognizing what is present in a scene: a planner must determine how road structure, surrounding agents, and their motion states should influence a future maneuver. Existing learning-based planners can capture these influences through latent scene features and trajectory decoders, but the relationship between environmental factors and candidate actions often remains implicit. This limits the ability to inspect, diagnose, or refine how scene context affects the safety of a predicted trajectory. Classical safety fields provide an explicit spatial representation of this relationship, but their risk shapes and relative weights are prescribed in advance and do not adapt to each scene. We introduce READ, a framework that learns an explicit, planning-aligned risk representation from complementary geometric and behavioral constraints. READ instantiates this representation as a continuous spatiotemporal field, enabling differentiable queries along candidate trajectories. The learned field connects scene understanding with action selection by encouraging predicted trajectories to align with low-risk regions, while retaining a differentiable interface for trajectory evaluation and refinement. READ integrates with both end-to-end planners and Vision-Language-Action models. Experiments on NAVSIM show consistent gains across matched end-to-end backbones and strong performance in a VLA setting; READ also achieves competitive results on NAVSIM v2. These results establish learned spatial risk as an explicit, adaptable representation for safe planning.

</details>


### [298] [Understanding Whole-Body Robot Teleoperation Strategies Under Diverse Task Objectives and Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12384)
*Tsung-Chi Lin,Juo-Tung Chen,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究复杂全身机器人遥操作的控制策略，提出混合控制框架并开展用户研究，验证协调控制能提升任务效率、降低关节极限风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身机器人遥操作需协调主动感知、双臂操作与移动，控制策略复杂；同时需要理解人在不同任务约束（如时间限制、低错误容忍）下的控制策略，以指导未来系统设计。

Method: 提出结合自由形式控制与约束控制的混合控制框架，用于TIAGo移动操作机器人的全身遥操作；通过用户研究考察不同任务约束下人的控制策略。

Result: 协调控制能有效提升任务效率，并降低达到单个关节极限的风险。

Conclusion: 讨论了结果及其对未来全身机器人遥操作系统设计的启示。

Abstract: This work investigates the control strategies of complex whole-body robot teleoperation that coordinate active perception, bimanual manipulation, and navigation. We developed a hybrid control framework, combining the free-form and constrained control, for the whole-body teleoperation of the TIAGo mobile manipulator. We conducted a user study to explore people's control strategies under different task constraints such as limited time and low tolerance of errors. Our results highlight the effective use of coordinated control in improving task efficiency and reducing the risk of reaching individual joint limits. We discuss our results and their implications for designing future whole-body robot teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [299] [FoldNet++: a Large-Scale Synthetic Dataset for Robotic T-Shirt Folding and Unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12433)
*Yuxing Chen,Zhiyuan Wei,Bowen Xiao,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模合成数据集FoldNetXX，覆盖6种机器人形态、1K件T恤、1K环境资产、120K带标注回合，仅用合成数据训练的策略可直接零样本迁移到真实环境，端到端任务成功率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衣物高度形变，导致机器人T恤折叠与展开的通用策略训练困难；真实数据采集成本高、标注难，缺乏跨形态、跨衣物、跨环境的大规模可泛化数据。

Method: 沿用FoldNet流程生成大规模可物理仿真、外观多样且带语义关键点标注的T恤数据集；基于语义关键点，通过统一规则框架为不同机器人形态生成操作演示；利用这些演示训练视觉运动策略。

Result: 仅基于该合成数据训练的模型，直接部署到未见过的真实环境和任意初始构型的未见过T恤时，端到端任务成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 大规模、多形态、富标注的合成数据可支撑视觉运动策略的零样本sim-to-real迁移，为解决可形变衣物操作的数据瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Due to the highly deformable nature of garments, training a generalizable policy for robotic T-shirt folding and unfolding remains a significant challenge. In this work, we present a large-scale synthetic dataset for robotic T-shirt folding and unfolding, covering 6 robotic embodiments, 1K T-shirts, 1K environmental assets, and 120K episodes with rich annotations, which can be used to train a wide range of manipulation policies. We first follow the FoldNet pipeline to generate a large-scale dataset of physically simulatable T-shirts with diverse appearances and annotated semantic keypoints. Based on these semantic keypoints, we then generate manipulation demonstrations for different robotic embodiments through a unified rule-based framework. We use these demonstrations to train visuomotor policies, and experimental results demonstrate that models trained solely on our synthetic data can achieve over 90\% end-to-end task success rates when directly deployed to unseen real-world environments and previously unseen T-shirts from arbitrary initial configurations. Project URL: https://pku-epic.github.io/FoldNetXX/.

</details>


### [300] [IMPLY: Physically Anchored Consistency for World-Model Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12441)
*Aman Mehta,Riya Baviskar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 世界模型的自洽性检查不足以验证其是否真正建模了物体物理属性，需将一致性锚定到物理证据上；作者提出IMPLY方法，通过反演模拟器读取每次rollout蕴含的物理量并评分，在受控与真实模型上均显著优于自洽性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于评估世界-动作模型（world-action models）的一致性检查只问模型的多个未来预测之间是否彼此一致，并不涉及任何物理知识。这导致一个内部建模了错误物体（错误质量/摩擦等）的模型，与建模了正确物体的模型在自洽性上表现完全相同，因而无法区分优劣，需要一种锚定到物理证据的评估方式。

Method: 提出IMPLY：通过反演一个模拟器，从每次rollout中读取其蕴含的物理量（如质量、摩擦），并以"同一个物体能否解释所有rollout"为准则对一组rollout进行评分；同时用模型已观测到的两个标定推（calibration pushes）作为锚点进行对照。

Result: 在受控设置中，自洽性会给一个忽略物体、总是预测典型推的模型满分，而锚定方法能将其识别出来（AUROC 0.70 对比 1.00）。在真实模型 V-JEPA 2-AC（适配到该场景）上同样如此：给定模型自身的标定推，它能跟踪物体（逐物体与真值相关性0.91）；给定另一物体的标定推则不能（0.05）。自洽性无法区分二者，仅以52%（随机水平）偏好正确证据，而锚定不一致性以73%偏好正确证据，并与rollout误差相关性达0.92–0.99。用于候选rollout集选择时，与能看到真值的oracle差距仅0.003。

Conclusion: 一个内部化了错误物体的模型与一个内部化了正确物体的模型在自洽性上完全等同，因此一致性必须锚定到证据上才能真正验证世界模型是否掌握了物体层面的物理规律。

Abstract: A world model asked what happens if an object is pushed at several speeds produces several futures. If the model has the object in mind, those futures agree about it: each implies the same mass and friction. The consistency checks now used to vet world-action models ask whether a model's futures agree with each other, and none of them knows any physics. We show that this is not enough, and what to do instead. IMPLY reads the physics each rollout implies by inverting a simulator and scores a set of rollouts by how well one object explains all of them, anchored to two calibration pushes the model has observed. In a controlled setting, self-consistency gives a perfect score to a model that ignores the object and always predicts a typical push; anchoring exposes it (AUROC 0.70 versus 1.00). On a real model, V-JEPA 2-AC adapted to the scene, the same thing happens. Given its own calibration pushes the model tracks the object (per-object correlation with the truth 0.91); given another object's, it does not (0.05). Self-consistency cannot tell these apart, preferring the right evidence on 52% of objects, chance level, while anchored disagreement prefers it on 73% and correlates 0.92-0.99 with the rollouts' error. Used to choose among candidate rollout sets, it comes within 0.003 of an oracle that sees the truth. A model that has internalised the wrong object is exactly as self-consistent as one that has internalised the right one; consistency has to be anchored to evidence.

</details>


### [301] [PATH: Continuous Target Sensing among Autonomous Cooperative Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12456)
*Heegyeong Kim,Alice James,Avishkar Seth,Endrowednes Kuantama,Jane Williamson,Yimeng Feng,Richard Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PATH——一种平台无关、几何辅助的无人机间目标交接与验证框架，通过RGB-D目标3D重建、相对位姿投影先验与跨视角双向握手验证实现跟踪责任转移，实验误差达厘米级，歧义场景下接收端采集准确率96.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机续航有限，持续目标跟踪需在协同无人机间转移跟踪责任。但交接要求接收端在视角、尺度、外观差异下识别同一物理目标。现有基于全局目标定位的方法受定位不确定性限制，基于外观的跨视角关联方法受视觉特征歧义限制，均难以可靠完成交接。

Method: PATH框架由三部分组成：(1) 发送端利用RGB-D传感将跟踪目标重建为度量3D点；(2) 接收端基于基准标记观测估计相对位姿，并将接收到的目标点投影到自身图像作为空间先验以完成目标获取；(3) 接收端生成的候选目标回传发送端，通过跨视角互一致性握手（Mutual Agreement Handshake）验证后才转移跟踪责任。框架平台无关，为几何辅助的感知与验证机制。

Result: 实机无人机实验显示平均相对位置误差0.047 m、目标位置误差0.030 m。在视觉歧义条件下，PATH实现96.0%的帧级接收端目标获取准确率，假阳性率2.0%、假阴性率2.0%。传感器误差敏感性分析表明相对位姿不确定性是接收视角投影误差的主导来源。系统以视频帧率运行，60 Hz下无人机间通信带宽低于16 kB/s。

Conclusion: PATH验证了在资源受限无人机平台上以轻量几何辅助方式实现目标交接的可行性，其精度高、通信开销小、对视觉歧义鲁棒，可支撑有限续航下的连续目标感知任务。

Abstract: Continuous target sensing by uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) is constrained by limited flight endurance, motivating the transfer of tracking responsibility between cooperating UAVs. Such a handoff requires the receiver to identify the same physical target currently tracked by the sender despite differences in viewpoint, scale, and target appearance. Existing approaches based on global target localization or appearance-based cross-view association are limited by positioning uncertainty or ambiguous visual features. This paper presents Perspective Alignment \& Tracking Handoff (\textbf{PATH}), a platform-agnostic, geometry-assisted sensing and verification framework for target handoff between two moving UAVs. The sender reconstructs the tracked target as a metric 3D point using RGB-D sensing, while the receiver estimates its relative pose from a fiducial observation and projects the transmitted target point into its own image as a spatial prior for target acquisition. The receiver-generated candidate is then returned to the sender and verified through a cross-view Mutual Agreement Handshake before tracking responsibility is transferred. Real-world UAV experiments show mean relative-position and target-position errors of 0.047~m and 0.030~m, respectively. Under visually ambiguous conditions, PATH achieves 96.0\% frame-level receiver-side target acquisition accuracy, with 2.0\% false-positive and 2.0\% false-negative rates. A sensor-error sensitivity analysis shows that relative-pose uncertainty is the dominant contributor to receiver-view projection error. The implementation operates at video rate with compact inter-UAV communication below 16~kB/s at 60~Hz, demonstrating the feasibility of lightweight geometry-assisted target handoff on resource-constrained UAV platforms.

</details>


### [302] [ArtManip: Category-Level Articulated In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12498)
*Yang Yang,Tengyu Liu,Puhao Li,Zeyuan Chen,Yuyang Li,Xingwan Wang,Yingying Wu,Zhaopeng Cui,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtManip，首个面向类别级关节物体手内操作方法，通过自动生成关节物体与功能性抓取的流程，以及包含关节物理随机化、奖励课程和潜在表征蒸馏的两阶段策略学习，实现跨实例与多样初始抓取的泛化，并在仿真和真实12个物体上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级关节物体手内操作对灵巧手极具挑战且研究不足。核心瓶颈有二：一是控制物体内部自由度与在自由浮动基座上维持抓取稳定性高度耦合；二是大规模获取多样物体模型和功能性抓取成本高昂，但系统对初始构型敏感，泛化又十分依赖这些数据。

Method: 方法分为两部分：初始构型构建方面，开发自动化流程，程序化生成多样关节物体并合成面向任务的功能性抓取；策略学习方面，提出稳健的两阶段训练策略，融合关节物理随机化、奖励课程以及潜在表征蒸馏，以应对部署中的复杂接触与关节动力学。

Result: 在四个物体类别上的大量实验表明，所提策略在仿真中可泛化到未见实例和多样构型，并实现零样本迁移至12个形状与关节机制各异的真实世界物体。

Conclusion: ArtManip是首个类别级关节物体手内操作方法，可跨物体实例与多样初始抓取泛化，为灵巧手操作关节物体提供了可扩展的生成式数据构建与稳健策略学习框架，并具备仿真到现实的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Category-level in-hand manipulation of articulated objects is a formidable yet underexplored challenge for dexterous robotic hands. This difficulty stems from two core bottlenecks: first, controlling an object's internal degrees of freedom is tightly coupled with maintaining grasp stability on a free-floating base; second, acquiring diverse object models and functional grasps at scale is highly labor-intensive, yet vital for generalization given the system's sensitivity to initial configurations. In this work, we present ArtManip, the first category-level articulated in-hand manipulation method that generalizes across object instances and diverse initial grasps. For initial configuration construction, we develop an automated pipeline that procedurally generates diverse articulated objects and synthesizes task-oriented functional grasps. For policy learning, we propose a robust two-stage training strategy that incorporates articulation physics randomization, reward curriculum, and latent representation distillation to handle complex contact and joint dynamics during deployment. Extensive experiments across four object categories demonstrate that our policy generalizes to unseen instances and varied configurations in simulation, and achieves zero-shot transfer to 12 real-world objects featuring diverse shapes and joint mechanics.

</details>


### [303] [Communication-Constrained Multi-Robot Exploration With Adaptive Communication Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12502)
*Ben Rossano,Jaein Lim,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MACE：一种去中心化多机器人探索框架，通过评估建立通信是否值得，在通信间歇场景下减少不必要行程，探索时间最多降低23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人并行探索可提升效率，但依赖有效的信息共享。当通信间歇时，机器人需权衡共享信息的收益与偏离探索去建立通信的成本。现有策略存在不足：纯机会式通信频率过低，固定会合策略则产生大量不必要行程。

Method: 提出去中心化探索框架MACE。在预定的通信窗口，机器人估计前往先前已识别通信位置的成本，将该决策建模为车辆定向问题（Vehicle Orienteering Problem）的变体，基于建立通信所需行程与沿途可完成的探索来评估路线，从而主动判断通信是否值得。

Result: 在多种不同规模与几何形状的模拟环境中，相比现有通信受限的探索策略，MACE将总探索时间最多减少23%；同时比纯机会式策略通信更频繁，并减少了固定会合策略带来的不必要行程。

Conclusion: 主动评估通信收益与成本，可在通信受限的多机器人探索中取得更优的通信—探索平衡，显著提升整体探索效率。

Abstract: Exploring unknown environments with multi-robot teams can improve efficiency by allowing robots to explore in parallel. However, realizing these gains requires effective information sharing. When communication is intermittent, robots must balance the benefits of sharing information against the cost of diverting from exploration to establish communication. This paper introduces MACE, a decentralized exploration framework that actively evaluates whether establishing communication is worthwhile. At scheduled communication windows, robots estimate the cost of reaching previously identified communication locations. By formulating this decision as a variant of the Vehicle Orienteering Problem, robots evaluate routes based on the travel required to establish communication and the exploration that can be completed along the way. This approach enables robots to communicate more frequently than under purely opportunistic strategies while reducing the unnecessary travel associated with fixed rendezvous strategies. Across a set of simulated environments with varying size and geometry, we demonstrate that MACE reduces the total exploration time by up to 23% compared to existing communication-constrained exploration strategies.

</details>


### [304] [Autonomous Precision Milling of Biological Structures via Generic Anatomical Priors and Active Boundary Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12530)
*Enduo Zhao,Xiaofeng Lin,Yifan Wang,Yuhan Song,Weihan Li,Saul Alexis Heredia Perez,Kanako Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种不确定性感知的自主铣削框架，通过通用解剖先验与主动边界感知的互补分工，实现生物结构的自主精密铣削。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主精密铣削生物结构面临目标几何、局部材料厚度及关键内部边界信息不完整的挑战；个体化术前模型虽可应对几何与厚度差异，但静态模型无法判断执行中遇到的边界状态，而反复进行目标特异性成像又限制了可扩展性。

Method: 框架为通用解剖先验与主动边界感知分配互补角色：通用先验提供保守的全局引导，经语义引导配准与视觉—力混合标定转化为面向个体目标的机器人可执行引导；当铣削接近不确定边界时，机器人主动探测剩余结构，利用相对刚度变化估计边界状态与结构可分离性；状态自适应控制器在主动感知与空间选择性增量精修之间切换，循环执行直至满足终止准则。

Result: 在生物替代物及活体小鼠颅窗制作上的分层实验表明，该方法可实现准确的解剖先验迁移、可靠的边界自适应以及生物结构的自主精密铣削。

Conclusion: 结合通用解剖先验与主动边界感知的不确定性感知框架，可在不过度依赖目标特异性成像的前提下，实现生物结构的自主精密铣削。

Abstract: Autonomous precision milling of biological structures is challenged by incomplete knowledge of target geometry, local material thickness, and critical internal boundaries. Subject-specific preoperative models can address geometric and thickness variations, but static models cannot determine boundary status encountered during execution, while repeated target-specific imaging limits scalability. This article presents an uncertainty-aware autonomous milling framework that assigns complementary roles to generic anatomical priors and active boundary perception. A generic anatomical prior provides conservative global guidance and is transformed through semantic-guided registration and hybrid vision-force calibration into robot-executable guidance for individual targets. As milling approaches uncertain boundaries, the robot actively probes the remaining structure and uses relative stiffness changes to estimate boundary status and structural detachability. A state-adaptive controller governs transitions between active perception and spatially selective incremental refinement, repeating this cycle until the termination criterion is satisfied. Hierarchical experiments on biological surrogates and in vivo mouse cranial window creation demonstrate accurate anatomical prior transfer, reliable boundary adaptation, and autonomous precision milling of biological structures.

</details>


### [305] [STAR: Sparse Tactile Representation Learning in Vision-Tactile-Language-Action Models for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12549)
*Xiangcheng Liu,Tianhao Wu,Le Zheng,Yidong Wang,Bowen Jiang,Mingjie Pan,Xinlin Ren,Yi Liu,Jianlan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了包含200小时双臂灵巧操作数据的大规模数据集（同步视觉、触觉与语言标注，10,576条轨迹、65个任务），并提出STAR训练方案，通过视觉-触觉联合预训练、稀疏全局触觉token表示与稀疏未来触觉预测，缓解触觉信号的时空与信息稀疏性，在四个真实任务上实现61%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要多指协调控制与有效触觉反馈，但学习这些能力面临两大挑战：缺乏大规模真实世界数据，以及难以从稀疏触觉信号中提取有效表征。

Method: 1) 搭建机器人平台与遥操作系统，采集200小时双臂灵巧操作数据集，含视觉、触觉与语言同步标注，涵盖65个任务、10,576条轨迹，其中69.5%涉及多指灵巧操作；2) 提出STAR——面向视觉-触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）模型的集成训练方案，针对触觉信号的空间、时间与信息稀疏性，采用视觉-触觉联合预训练、稀疏全局触觉token表示、稀疏未来触觉预测三项技术。

Result: 在该数据集上训练后，STAR在四个真实世界任务上、每任务100条后训练轨迹的设定下，取得61%的平均成功率；在任务特定后训练下展现出灵巧操作性能。

Conclusion: 大规模同步多模态灵巧操作数据与针对性缓解触觉稀疏性的训练方案相结合，可有效支撑VTLA模型实现真实场景下的灵巧操作，为触觉驱动的操作学习提供了数据与方法的双重基础。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires coordinated multi-finger control and effective tactile feedback, yet learning these capabilities remains challenging due to the lack of large-scale real-world data and the difficulty of extracting effective representations from sparse tactile signals. We build a robot platform and teleoperation system to collect a 200-hour bimanual dexterous manipulation dataset with synchronized visual, tactile, and language annotations, comprising 10,576 trajectories across 65 tasks, 69.5% of which involve dexterous multi-finger manipulation. We further propose STAR, an integrated training recipe for vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) models that addresses the spatial, temporal, and informational sparsity of tactile signals through visual-tactile joint pre-training, sparse-global tactile token representation, and sparse future tactile prediction. Trained on this dataset, STAR achieves a 61% average success rate across four real-world tasks with 100 post-training trajectories per task, demonstrating dexterous performance under task-specific post-training.

</details>


### [306] [A Hierarchical Coverage Path Planning Algorithm for Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12595)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Hongbin Ma,Yaming Ou,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向未知环境的在线覆盖路径规划算法，通过增量构建的分解树组织子区域，并在线维护全局覆盖路径，在高保真仿真中相比三种基线算法提升了覆盖效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境中的覆盖路径规划需要机器人在环境信息逐步揭示的过程中实时调整轨迹，现有方法在路径长度和重叠率方面仍有改进空间。

Method: 将初始未知的搜索区域随障碍信息获取与覆盖推进逐步分解为互不连通的子区域，并以增量构建的分解树保留其父子层级关系；基于该树维护并在线更新全局覆盖路径，依据子区域的探索状态与机器人距离优先处理新生成的子区域；再由局部规划器在选定子区域内生成覆盖运动。

Result: 在复杂场景的高保真仿真中，相比三种基线算法，所提方法在路径长度和重叠率两项指标上均实现了覆盖效率的提升。

Conclusion: 基于分解树与分层全局规划、局部规划相结合的在线覆盖路径规划框架，能够有效适应环境逐步揭示的过程并提升覆盖效率。

Abstract: This paper presents an online coverage path planning algorithm for unknown environments. During navigation, the initially unknown search area is progressively decomposed into disconnected subareas as new obstacle information is acquired and coverage proceeds. These subareas are organized in an incrementally constructed decomposition tree that preserves their hierarchical parent-child relationships. Based on this tree, a global coverage tour is maintained and updated online by prioritizing newly generated child subareas according to their exploration states and distances from the robot. A local planner then generates coverage motions within each selected subarea, allowing the robot to adapt its trajectory as the environment is gradually revealed. Its performance is evaluated via high-fidelity simulations in complex scenarios. The results show improved coverage efficiency in terms of path length and overlap ratio in comparison to three baseline algorithms.

</details>


### [307] [Quantifying Spectral Differences in Vehicle Between Production Autonomous and Human-Driven Vehicles Across Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12609)
*Peiyi Fang,Xiangyu Li,Yonglin Weng,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究基于真实世界PAV数据集（四个平台），构建频域分析框架，量化PAV与HV在不同驾驶情景下的运动学差异，发现差异具有明显情景依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAV-HV运动学差异研究多依赖仿真模型或受控实验中的低阶ACC系统，且常用时域指标，难以反映数据驱动黑箱式高阶自动驾驶行为及不同情景下的频率相关交通动态。

Method: 采用含四个PAV平台的真实数据集，将运动学信号变换至频域并提取频谱特征，基于核密度估计与Wasserstein距离比较PAV与HV在驾驶状态、光照、天气和车辆密度等多情景下的差异。

Result: PAV-HV频谱差异呈明显情景依赖性：跟驰状态下速度相关差异普遍小于巡航状态；雨天条件相较晴天持续放大加速度相关差异。

Conclusion: 研究强调多情景评估的必要性，并证明频域分析在真实条件下刻画PAV-HV运动学差异方面的价值。

Abstract: Differences in vehicle kinematic characteristics between production autonomous vehicles (PAVs) and human-driven vehicles (HVs) have been limitedly investigated by empirical studies. Most recent studies rely on simulation-based models, while some further investigate low-level adaptive cruise control (ACC) systems in controlled experiments. These methods commonly adapt some time-domain metrics to characterize PAV-HV differences across limited driving conditions. However, current PAVs equipped with high-level autonomous driving systems generate driving behaviors in a black box using data-driven models. These fundamentally different mechanisms for generating behaviors may produce distinct kinematic characteristics in traffic. More importantly, these time-domain metrics cannot reflect frequency-related traffic dynamics across different driving scenarios. Thus, this study adapted a real-world PAV dataset with four PAV platforms and developed a frequency-domain framework to quantify kinematic differences between PAVs and HVs across diverse driving scenarios, including varying driving states, lighting, weather, and vehicle densities. The framework transforms kinematic signals into the frequency domain and extracts spectral features, and then compares these features between PAVs and HVs based on kernel density estimation and Wasserstein distance. The results reveal clear scenario-dependent PAV-HV spectral differences. Specifically, speed-related differences were consistently smaller during car-following than cruising, while rainy conditions consistently enlarged acceleration-related differences compared with clear conditions. These findings highlight the necessity of multi-scenario evaluations and demonstrate the value of frequency-domain analysis for characterizing PAV-HV kinematic differences under real-world conditions.

</details>


### [308] [ProClosure: Hierarchical Room-Object Assignment using Progressive Boundary Closure from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12614)
*Vinoth Kumar Muthuraj,Soumyadeep Banik,Kushal Sharma,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出渐进边界闭合(Progressive Boundary Closure)方法，从单目RGB视频恢复房间层，解决墙体未观测缺口导致的房间错误合并问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景图中房间分组决定机器人能否按房间名检索物体；物体记入错误房间将无法检索。核心难点在于俯视地图中墙体只在相机看过的位置被记录，边界缺口既可能是门洞也可能是未观测的墙段，二者无法区分，先前方法一律视为通道，导致本应分开的房间被合并。

Method: 关键洞见：无论缺口是门洞还是未观测墙段，房间都不应跨越它，故统一予以闭合、无需区分。依据两点——这类开口在少量边界增长下即可封闭；且穿过它的视线较少，故不同房间的点彼此很少可见。前者用于恢复房间，后者用于物体归属。房间恢复通过将边界渐进向内增厚，一旦某自由空间区域被完全包围即冻结，使每个开口在其自身尺度上封闭，而非预先固定半径；并以相机位姿作为种子，消除采样启发式，使分割具有确定性。

Result: 在6个HM3D-Semantics场景的10层楼上，与HOV-SG在相同俯视地图上对比：本文恢复74个房间对应72个标注区域(HOV-SG为44个)，IoU 0.25下房间F_1由0.741提升至0.890(精度略有代价)，物体到房间的ARI由0.488提升至0.696(p=0.002，且每层均领先)。

Conclusion: 渐进边界闭合以统一对待所有边界缺口、自适应尺度封闭开口的确定性方案，显著提升了房间分割与物体—房间归属质量，为基于单目视频的3D场景图构建提供了更可靠的房间层恢复途径。

Abstract: A 3D scene graph groups objects into rooms. When a robot is asked to fetch an object from the kitchen, that grouping is what tells it where to look. An object recorded in the wrong room is not retrievable by a query naming the correct room. We introduce Progressive Boundary Closure, which recovers room layer from a monocular RGB video. A SLAM front end and an open-vocabulary segmenter supply a structural point cloud, camera trajectory and object tracks. The cloud is rasterised into a top-down map, rooms are recovered from it, and each object takes the room holding most of its extent. The difficulty lies in the map itself. Walls are recorded only where the camera looked, so a gap in the boundary may be a doorway or a stretch of wall that was never observed; nothing distinguishes the two. Prior methods treat both as passages, merging rooms that should remain separate. We observe that both require the same treatment: a room should not extend across either, so both are closed and need not be distinguished. Such an opening closes under a small amount of boundary growth, and few sightlines cross it, so points in different rooms rarely see one another. We use the first to recover rooms and the second to assign objects to them. Rooms are obtained by Progressively thickening the boundary inward and freezing each free-space region once it becomes enclosed, so every opening seals at its own scale rather than at a radius fixed in advance. Camera poses are used as seeds, which removes the sampling heuristic and makes the segmentation deterministic. Over 10 floors of 6 HM3D-Semantics scenes, scored against HOV-SG on identical top-down maps, we recover 74 rooms for 72 annotated regions (HOV-SG: 44), raising room F_1 from 0.741 to 0.890 at IoU 0.25 at some cost in precision, and object-to-room ARI from 0.488 to 0.696 (p=0.002, ahead on every floor).

</details>


### [309] [Online Material Estimation for Conditioned Diffusion Policy in Shaping Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12634)
*Ryunosuke Yamada,Tomohiro Motoda,Yukiyasu Domae,Tokuo Tsuji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以在线估计的材料标签为条件的扩散策略，用于可变形线性物体（DLO）形状控制，无需先验材料信息即可达到与真值标签同等的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO的变形行为随刚度、弹性等材料属性变化，即使目标形状相同，单一策略也需为不同物体生成不同动作序列，因此模仿学习中的形状控制十分困难；仅以任务为条件的策略难以泛化到多材料场景。

Method: 1）用循环估计网络从多视角图像时序与机器人关节状态中在线预测被夹持物体的材料标签；2）将该预测标签在每个推理步对扩散策略进行条件化。采集480条真机演示，涵盖4种DLO材料与3种沟槽放置任务；对比每组材料专用专家策略、无材料标签的任务条件策略、以真值材料标签为条件的策略以及所提策略。

Result: 以真值材料标签条件化将平均成功率由45.8%（仅任务条件）提升至60.0%；所提策略在无任何先验材料信息下达到60.8%，与真值标签策略持平。事后分析表明估计器能从操作观测中提取材料相关信息，扩散策略对该条件信号有响应；唯一显著失败案例源于两种相似材料间的持续混淆。

Conclusion: 在线估计的材料标签可作为扩散策略的有效条件，使DLO形状控制策略在材料属性未知时仍能自适应不同材料，性能接近使用真值标签的上界，验证了材料感知条件化对变形物体操作的价值。

Abstract: Shape control of deformable linear objects (DLOs) is challenging for imitation learning because deformation behavior varies with material properties such as stiffness and elasticity, so a single policy must generate different action sequences for different objects even when the goal shape is identical. We propose a diffusion policy conditioned on material labels that are estimated online during manipulation. A recurrent estimation network predicts the material label of the grasped object from the time series of multi-view images and robot joint states, and the predicted label conditions the diffusion policy at every inference step. We collected 480 real-robot demonstrations covering four DLO materials and three groove-placement tasks, and compared per-material specialist policies, a task-conditioned policy without material labels, a policy conditioned on ground-truth material labels, and the proposed policy. Conditioning on ground-truth material labels improved the average success rate from 45.8% to 60.0% over the task-only policy, and the proposed policy reached 60.8% without any prior material information, matching the policy given ground-truth labels. A post-hoc analysis shows that the estimator extracts material-related information from the manipulation observations and that the diffusion policy responds to the resulting conditioning signal, while the one pronounced failure case is associated with persistent confusion between two similar materials.

</details>
