### [310] [Driving Context-guided Model Predictive Planning and Control for Autonomous Car Racing at the Limit and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12660)
*Ayoub Raji,Federico Sacco,Nicola Musiu,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于MPC的自动驾驶赛车运动规划与控制流水线（MPP+MPC），通过Cost Blending状态机识别驾驶场景并切换权重，实车验证圈速接近人类最佳车手2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车需在极限操控下适应超车、常规行驶、反打方向等不同驾驶场景，单一固定的规划与控制参数难以兼顾，因此需要能够识别场景并动态调整目标权重的统一框架。

Method: 采用基于模型预测控制的运动规划与控制流水线；规划模块（MPP）为开环优化，控制模块（MPC）为闭环优化，二者共享同一问题建模与模型，仅在预测时域长度、求解频率、参数整定及开/闭环方式上不同；由Cost Blending状态机识别当前驾驶场景，并选择对应的预定义权重施加于MPP与MPC。

Result: 在完全自动驾驶的开轮式赛车Superformula EAV-25上完成实车验证，圈速与最佳人类车手参考值差距在2%以内；可稳定在操控极限行驶，平顺完成超车动作，并在高转向过度工况下快速反应以恢复车辆稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的共享建模、双模块协同+场景权重混合的MPC架构，能有效统一多驾驶场景下的赛车规划与控制，并在实车上达到接近人类顶尖车手的极限性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a Model Predictive Control-based motion planning and control pipeline for autonomous car racing capable of adapting to different driving contexts, such as overtaking, nominal driving, and countersteering. A Cost Blending state machine manages the identification of different driving contexts and the selection of their predefined weights to be applied to the Model Predictive Planning (MPP) and Control (MPC) modules. The two optimization-based solutions share the same problem formulation and model, differing only in horizon length, rate, tuning, and in their open-loop versus closed-loop approach to maximize the effectiveness of their interaction. The work is validated on the fully autonomous open-wheel racecar Superformula EAV-25, with a lap time achieved that is within 2% of the best human driver reference. The results demonstrate the capability of the solution in driving at the limit of handling, smoothly executing overtaking maneuvers, and quickly reacting to high oversteering conditions to recover the vehicle stability.

</details>


### [311] [Improving Imitation Learning Efficiency for Manipulation through Geometric Prior Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12721)
*Shogo Iwakata,Tomohiro Motoda,Ryosuke Yamada,Koshi Makihara,Ryoichi Nakajo,Keitaro Tanaka,Masaki Murooka,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Shigeo Morishima,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种极简几何视觉预训练数据集（仅含平面、物体与手，轨迹自动生成，手用立方体表示），以此为策略提供低成本先验。基于ACT在3个仿真机器人（各5个任务）和3个真实任务上验证：在少量任务演示下，由该几何先验微调比从零训练在训练早期成功率更高，且能跨机器人并迁移至真实任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习应用于新操作任务通常需重新收集演示并重训模型，样本效率成为实际瓶颈。大规模机器人数据预训练虽有效但采集与训练成本高，数据增强也往往需针对每个任务重新生成数据并重训。因此作者关注一个互补问题：在采集任何任务特定数据之前，能否以可忽略的成本为策略提供有用的先验。

Method: 构建几何视觉预训练数据集：每个场景仅包含平面、物体和手，轨迹自动生成；场景不含纹理与背景，预训练主要让策略接触手与物体间的几何关系。用立方体表示手以避免数据集绑定特定机器人形态。以ACT为策略，在3个仿真机器人（各5个任务）及3个真实机器人任务上评估该几何先验。

Result: 在许多机器人—任务组合中，使用少量任务演示时，从几何先验微调在训练早期阶段即可获得比从零训练更高的成功率；该初始化可跨机器人迁移，并在任务数据有限时迁移到真实世界任务。

Conclusion: 即使高度简化的几何场景也能提供有用的初始化，在任务数据有限的情况下跨机器人泛化并迁移至真实任务。

Abstract: Applying an imitation learning policy to a new manipulation task usually requires collecting new demonstrations and retraining the model, which makes sample efficiency a practical concern. Pretraining on large-scale robot datasets is effective in this respect, but such datasets are costly to collect and train on, while data augmentation techniques typically require a new round of data generation and retraining for each task. A complementary question is what useful prior can be provided to a policy at negligible cost before any task-specific data are collected. In this study, we construct a geometric visual pretraining dataset in which each scene contains only a plane, an object, and a hand, and trajectories are generated automatically. The scenes contain neither textures nor backgrounds; pretraining primarily exposes the policy to the geometric relationship between the hand and the object. Furthermore, representing the hand as a cube avoids tailoring the dataset to a specific robot morphology. We evaluate this geometric prior using ACT on three simulated robots across five manipulation tasks each, as well as on three real-world robot tasks. Across many of these robot--task combinations, fine-tuning from the geometric prior achieves higher success rates in the early stages of training than training from scratch while using only a small number of task demonstrations. These results suggest that even highly simplified geometric scenes can provide a useful initialization that transfers across robots and to real-world tasks when task data are limited.

</details>


### [312] [Control Architecture for Safe Grasping of Fragile Objects Using a Coarse Position-Controlled Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12737)
*Marko Pavlic,Moritz Geier,Timo Markert,Darius Burschka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的力/力矩接触估计方法，结合自适应导纳控制器，使位置控制型夹爪在抓取未知物体时实现柔顺、稳定且不过力的安全抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化环境中作业日益增多，安全抓取未知物体且不造成损伤成为关键需求。人类通过感知并快速调节抓取力来实现这一点，而机器人同样需要柔顺的交互策略，以适应不确定的物体属性并在操作过程中应对各种不稳定因素。

Method: 针对粗糙位置控制的夹爪，提出几何感知的基于力/力矩的接触估计方法，并配合自适应导纳控制器进行安全抓取。接触力期望值在线估计，以与未知属性物体保持稳定接触，从而在避免过力的同时实现柔顺且稳定的抓取。

Result: 使用不同尺寸、形状、刚度与重量的物体进行实验，结果表明该算法既能防止滑移，又能以最小力安全抓取物体，且不引起过度变形。

Conclusion: 几何感知接触估计与自适应导纳控制相结合，可在未知物体抓取中实现防滑、低力、无过度变形的安全柔顺抓取，验证了方法的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly used in unstructured environments. The need for them to safely grasp unknown objects without damaging them becomes crucial. Humans achieve this by sensing and quickly responding by adjusting their grasping force. Similarly, effective grasp acquisition in robots requires compliant interaction strategies that can adapt to uncertain object properties and adjust to any instabilities during manipulation.
  We present a geometry-aware force/torque-based contact estimation method for a coarse position-controlled gripper, combined with an adaptive admittance controller for safe grasp acquisition. The desired contact forces are estimated online to keep stable contact with objects of unknown properties. This enables compliant and stable grasps while avoiding excessive forces. Experiments with objects of different sizes, shapes, stiffnesses, and weights show that the proposed algorithm not only prevents slippage but also applies minimal force to safely grasp an object without causing excessive deformation.

</details>


### [313] [High-Fidelity Multi-Body Simulator for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12795)
*Nicola Musiu,Francesco Iacovacci,Fausto Lupo,Matteo Pini,Giovanni Scapicchi,Francesco Moretti,Eugenio Mascaro,Pietro Musso,Ayoub Raji,Marko Bertogna,Vincenzo Maria Arricale,Angelo Lo Sapio,Alessandro Piccarelli,Garron Fish*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于自动驾驶赛车软件测试与验证的高保真车辆动力学仿真环境：Dymola多体数字孪生+CRG三维路面，导出FMU并集成到自研软件在环仿真器，含标定与验证，可在便携机上实时运行并提供可靠真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车算法在实车部署前需要高保真、可实时运行的验证平台，以降低风险与成本；现有仿真环境在车辆动力学精度、路面细节与实时性上难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 在Dymola中基于赛车动力学建模库构建完整多体数字孪生；2) 使用CRG标准实现含高程轮廓与路缘石的三维路面；3) 将模型导出为FMU，集成到自研软件在环（SIL）仿真器，并用C++开发与自动驾驶赛车软件栈的通信接口；4) 提出基于实验数据的标定流程并进行验证研究。

Result: 仿真器可在便携式计算机上实时运行，并通过标定与验证研究验证了框架质量，能够为自动驾驶赛车算法验证提供可靠的真值（ground truth）。

Conclusion: 该高保真、可实时运行的SIL仿真框架可作为实车部署前的有效算法验证手段，兼具建模精度、路面细节与工程可用性。

Abstract: We present a custom high-fidelity vehicle dynamics simulation environment for testing and validation of Autonomous Racing software. The digital twin of the autonomous vehicle is developed in Dymola, using racecar dynamics modeling libraries to build a complete multi-body model. A 3D road surface, including elevation profiles and curbs, is implemented using the Curved Regular Grid (CRG) standard. The model is exported from Dymola as a Functional Mock-up Unit (FMU) and integrated into a custom software-in-the-loop simulator, where communication interfaces with the autonomous racing stack were developed in C++. A calibration procedure based on experimental data is also presented, along with a validation study to further support the quality of the proposed framework. The simulator runs in real time on a portable computer and provides reliable ground truth for algorithms validation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [314] [VertexCBF: Improving Neural Control Barrier Functions via Vertex-Restricted Control Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12831)
*Bojan Derajić,Sebastian Bernhard,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VertexCBF框架，以可扩展、系统化且可解释的方式学习神经控制屏障函数(CBF)，通过物理信息与稀疏监督学习逼近稳态Hamilton–Jacobi值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主机器人数量增长，安全性愈发重要。CBF虽提供有理论保障的安全框架，但现有设计方法在有效性、可扩展性或可解释性上存在局限，且常导致过于保守的安全集。

Method: 利用控制仿射动力学与凸多面体控制集（此时Hamiltonian在控制顶点处取最大），通过GPU并行顶点受限树搜索高效生成监督点；采用残差架构保证所学CBF不大于指定约束函数；结合物理信息学习与稀疏监督学习训练神经网络逼近稳态Hamilton–Jacobi值函数。

Result: 在15个系统上与相关基线对比，本方法能可靠恢复较大的安全集，而基线方法往往保守甚至完全失败。此外进行了硬件实验，移动机器人使用本方法训练的神经CBF安全避让行人。

Conclusion: VertexCBF为神经CBF学习提供了可扩展、系统化且可解释的框架，能有效扩大安全集并具备实际硬件可行性。

Abstract: As the number of autonomous robots continues to grow, safety becomes increasingly important. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a theoretically grounded framework for ensuring safety, but existing design methods often face limitations in effectiveness, scalability, or interpretability, and may result in overly conservative safe sets. In this paper, we propose \emph{VertexCBF}, a framework for learning neural CBFs in a scalable, systematic, and explainable way. We approximate the stationary Hamilton--Jacobi value function using a neural network trained via a combination of physics-informed and sparsely supervised learning. By exploiting control-affine dynamics and a convex polytope control set, under which the Hamiltonian is maximized at the control vertices, we efficiently generate supervision points via GPU-parallel vertex-restricted tree search, while a residual architecture guarantees that the learned CBF is never larger than the specified constraint function. We evaluate the method on 15 systems and compare it against relevant baselines, showing that it reliably recovers large safe sets where the baselines are conservative or fail completely. In addition, we perform a hardware experiment in which a mobile robot safely avoids pedestrians using a neural CBF trained with our method.

</details>


### [315] [Parameter Sensitivity Analysis for Aerial LiDAR-Inertial Odometries in low-altitude flights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12837)
*Robert Milijas,Jose Ramiro Martinez-de Dios,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析FAST-LIO2与Cartographer的LIO模块参数对空中LiDAR SLAM性能(ATE)的影响，通过网格搜索、Pearson相关与随机森林置换重要性，识别关键参数，提出简化调参流程与建议，使94%的案例在5 cm内接近最优ATE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR SLAM与LIO算法是无人机精确导航的关键，但其性能高度依赖场景、LiDAR类型与机器人运动特性，通常需要大量繁琐调参才能达到理想效果。论文旨在减少调参工作量，阐明各参数对性能的影响规律。

Method: 在空中LiDAR SLAM数据集（不同LiDAR、低至中等高度飞行、多样环境）上，对基于EKF的FAST-LIO2和基于图优化的Cartographer的LIO模块进行穷举网格搜索，遍历参数组合并计算绝对轨迹误差(ATE)；用Pearson相关分析单参数与ATE的关系，并训练随机森林模型预测ATE，通过置换重要性评估各参数影响力。

Result: 对Cartographer与FAST-LIO2均得到：i) 识别出对性能影响较大的参数；ii) 一套简化的调参流程；iii) 具体的调参建议。依据所提建议配置时，两个算法在94%的测试案例中ATE达到与网格搜索最优值相差5 cm以内的水平。

Conclusion: 参数影响力分析可有效指导LiDAR SLAM/LIO的调参，所提简化流程与建议能在几乎不损失精度的前提下显著降低调参成本，为空中机器人导航部署提供实用参考。

Abstract: LiDAR-based SLAM (Simultaneous Localization and Mapping) and LIO (LiDAR-inertial odometry) algorithms are often used for precise navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles, especially during interactions with the aerial robot's environment. However, the performance of these algorithms is greatly dependent on the scenario, LiDAR, and robot motion characteristics, often requiring an intensive tuning process to achieve the desired performance. To aid these tuning efforts, this paper analyzes the influence on performance of the parameters of an EKF-based LIO algorithm (FAST-LIO2) and the LIO module of a graph-based SLAM algorithm (Cartographer) on aerial LiDAR SLAM datasets recorded using different LiDARs in low-to-moderate-altitude flights in diverse environments. The analysis is conducted on the absolute trajectory error (ATE) resulting from processing the datasets with the LIO algorithms configured with each combination of parameters obtained in an exhaustive grid search. The relationship between individual parameters and the ATE results is assessed using Pearson's correlation, while the influence of each parameter is assessed using random forest permutation importance analyses with random forest models trained to predict the resulting ATE values based on the choice of parameters. The performed analysis obtains for Cartographer and FAST-LIO2: i) the identification of parameters with stronger influence in performance, ii) a simplified tuning procedure, and iii) tuning recommendations. Using the proposed tuning recommendations, both algorithms obtain on the analyzed datasets ATE values within 5 cm to the optimal performance found in the grid search procedure in 94% of the analyzed cases.

</details>


### [316] [A Multi-Vehicle Dataset with Camera, LiDAR, and Radar Sensors and Scanned 3D Models for Custom Auto-Annotation using RTK-GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12871)
*Philipp Berthold,Bianca Forkel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个面向感知测量原理评估的自动驾驶数据集，包含7辆目标车的扫描3D模型及RTK-GNSS位姿与连续运动学参考，可生成用户自定义粒度的标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集通常只提供语义分割、点关联或边界框标注，难以深入评估传感器的测量原理与测量效应。作者希望构建能刻画传感器车辆完整动态环境状态的数据集，以支持对遮挡、反射等现象的细致分析。

Method: 采集7辆目标车的扫描式3D模型，并用RTK-GNSS记录其位姿与连续运动学参考；包含单目标与多目标记录；由于目标车表面法线已知，可据此推导测量效应并生成用户自定义粒度的后续参考格式。

Result: 构建了覆盖单/多目标场景的数据集，可支持遮挡、反射等测量效应的评估；论文描述了数据集构成、技术背景，并给出了示例性评估。

Conclusion: 该数据集从测量原理层面补充了现有自动驾驶感知数据集，为传感器与目标特性分析及后续参考格式的灵活生成提供了基础。

Abstract: Datasets are a crucial element in the development of perception algorithms. They relate sensor measurement data to annotated reference information and allow for the deduction of sensor and object characteristics. In autonomous driving, the reference data commonly consist of semantic image segmentation, point-wise associations, or bounding box annotations. The dataset proposed in this work, however, aims to dig deeper into the evaluation of measurement principles and provides scanned 3D models of all vehicles together with a pose and continuous kinematics reference obtained by RTK-GNSS. Combined, the state of the complete dynamic surrounding of the sensor vehicle is known for any point in time. Subsequent reference formats can be easily computed in user-defined granularity. This dataset involves single-object and multi-object recordings with seven target vehicles. In particular, measurement effects such as occlusion, as well as reflections, can be evaluated, as the normals of the shape of the target vehicles are known. We describe the dataset, discuss the technical background of its development, and briefly present exemplary evaluations.

</details>


### [317] [From Transportation to Manipulation: Enabling Grasping in Magnetic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12883)
*Lara Bergmann,Noah Greis,Cedric Grothues,Lisa-Marie Weigelt,Klaus Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gripper MagBot：一种低成本并联 6-DoF 操作器，集成 1-DoF 夹爪，通过机械耦合三个磁悬浮动子，实现磁悬浮系统上的抓取与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁悬浮系统因可扩展性与灵活性，在高混合、低产量制造中具有潜力，可实现高可重构的机内物料流。但其操作能力尚未被充分开发，现有应用几乎只聚焦于运输。若要在磁悬浮系统上直接抓取与操作，通常需额外昂贵的手持设备（如工业机械臂），因此需要一种无需额外设备即可实现抓取与操作的低成本方案。

Method: 提出 Gripper MagBot：低成本并联 6-DoF 操作器，集成 1-DoF 夹爪，并机械耦合三个磁悬浮动子。支持两种工作配置：默认模式与单轨模式，可按所需稳定性与工作空间占用选择。整机可通过对接站自主下放与拾取以完成重构。采用逆运动学控制器进行控制。

Result: 在仿真中展示了抓取放置示例，并在真实 Gripper MagBot 上使用逆运动学控制器进行了验证。CAD 文件、装配说明、组件清单与视频见 https://sites.google.com/view/gripper-magbot。

Conclusion: Gripper MagBot 以低成本方式将抓取与操作能力直接引入磁悬浮系统，避免额外工业机械臂等昂贵设备，并通过双配置与可自主对接重构提升制造灵活性与可重构性。

Abstract: Magnetic levitation (MagLev) systems have great potential for application in high-mix, low-volume manufacturing due to their scalability and flexibility, enabling highly reconfigurable in-machine material flow. However, their manipulation capabilities remain largely unexploited, as current applications almost exclusively focus on transportation. To enable grasping and manipulation directly on MagLev systems without requiring additional costly handling equipment, such as industrial robot arms, we present the Gripper MagBot, a low-cost parallel 6-DoF manipulator with an integrated 1-DoF gripper that mechanically couples three MagLev movers. The Gripper MagBot supports two operating configurations: a default mode and a single-track mode, selectable depending on the required stability and workspace footprint. To reconfigure a machine, the MagBot can be autonomously dropped off and picked up using a docking station. We showcase pick-and-place examples in simulation, as well as with the real Gripper MagBot using our inverse kinematics controller. CAD files, assembly instructions, a component list, and videos are available at https://sites.google.com/view/gripper-magbot.

</details>


### [318] [Before the Tipping Point: Force-Guided Active Perception for Shape-Agnostic Estimation of 3D Centers of Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12894)
*Steven M. Hyland,Jing Xiao,Cagdas D. Onal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于力的方法，通过机器人单次亚临界倾覆实验估计未知物体的质心高度与质量，相对误差低于5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法抓取、几何形状不规则或质量分布不均的情况下，估计未知物体的三维质心极具挑战性。

Method: 机器人施加准静态抬升推-退运动，利用倾覆过程中的力-角度测量数据从物体轨迹中辨识参数；推-退循环可减弱摩擦偏置，实现泛化拟合；并借助安全裕度防止翻转，使物体保持亚临界倾覆状态。

Result: 在配备六轴力扭矩传感器的机械臂上，对多种无先验形状信息、无特定模型的物体进行实验，质量、质心高度和倾覆角的相对误差均低于5.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在适当安全阈值下可实现可靠的三维惯性参数估计，有助于实现可靠的非抓取操作及对以往难以处理物体的机器人抓取。

Abstract: Estimating the 3D center of mass of unknown objects is challenging when grasping is infeasible, geometry is irregular, or mass distribution is uneven. We present a force-based method that estimates CoM height and mass from a single sub-critical tipping experiment by a robot manipulator. The robot applies a quasistatic elevated push and retract motion, using force-angle measurements recorded during tipping to identify parameters from the object trajectory. Our proposed push-retract cycle mitigates frictional bias, enabling generalized fitting. We experimentally validate our method using a robot manipulator with a six-axis force torque sensor on varying types of objects without prior shape information and without specific models. We also propose a method to prevent toppling, keeping the object in a sub-critical tipping regime by leveraging a safety margin. In experimental studies, our method recovers mass, CoM height, and toppling angle with relative errors below 5.0 percent across all unknown objects. This work demonstrates reliable 3D inertial parameter estimation under proper safety thresholds in tipping. Our proposed method informs and enables reliable non-prehensile manipulation and robotic grasping of challenging objects that were previously infeasible.

</details>


### [319] [Robust Underwater Grasping of Sloped Objects with a Waterproof Passive Adaptive Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12927)
*Jooyoung Hong,Daewon Hong,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种防水被动自适应夹爪，结合被动旋转关节与变刚度软垫，实现干湿环境下对圆柱/圆锥物体的稳定抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行夹爪抓取日常物体时，面对斜面或非对称物体易产生力矩驱动的滚动与剪切滑移；在厨房等涉水家用场景中，物体常处于湿润或浸没状态，摩擦系数大幅降低，抓取难度进一步加剧。现有水下机器人抓取方案难以满足日常生活场景的稳定抓取需求，存在明显空白。

Method: 设计全防水被动自适应夹爪，兼顾局部与全局自适应能力：引入被动旋转关节，使夹爪在斜面物体上实现全局自对准；采用被动变刚度软垫，实现对物体表面的局部形状适应，两者结合提升接触稳定性与抗滑移能力。

Result: 在干燥与水下两种条件下，对多种圆柱与圆锥形物体开展抓取实验，结果显示所提夹爪在保持力与抓取鲁棒性方面均优于刚性软垫基线与固定关节基线。

Conclusion: 该设计为水下日常物体的稳定操作提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案，弥补了当前水下机器人抓取在日常应用场景中的关键不足。

Abstract: Robust grasping of everyday objects remains challenging for parallel-jaw grippers, particularly when handling sloped or asymmetric items that induce torque-driven rolling and shear slip. These challenges become even more severe in domestic environments such as kitchens, where objects are often wet or submerged, drastically reducing friction between the gripper and the object. To address these issues, we present a waterproof passive adaptive gripper that combines local and global adaptability for high-performance grasping in the submerged environments. The proposed gripper features a fully waterproof design that integrates a passive rotational joint for global self-alignment on sloped surfaces and passive variable-stiffness pads for local surface adaptation. Experiments in both dry and underwater conditions on various cylindrical and conical objects demonstrate superior holding capability and grasp robustness compared to rigid-pad and fixed-joint baselines. The proposed design offers a practical and robust solution that successfully enables stable underwater manipulation of diverse everyday objects, effectively addressing a critical gap in current underwater robotic grasping for daily-life applications.

</details>


### [320] [A Robot Among People:From Social Imitation to the Social Becoming of Human Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12937)
*Victor Tuan Vu Pham,Judith Dörrenbächer,Thomas H. Weisswange,Marc Hassenzahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文主张跳出"解决方案主义"陷阱，将公共空间中的机器人理解为共享环境中的情境化要素，是群体经验的催化剂与载体，其意义通过人们的定位、解释和互动而生成，而非离散的社交智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体中介机器人研究常陷入"解决方案主义"陷阱，把机器人预设为修复冲突、脱离参与、缺乏协调等问题的社交智能体。作者认为这一框架存在根本局限，需要一种新的理解方式。

Method: 概念性/理论性论述：提出将机器人视为共享环境中的情境化要素，作为群体经验的催化剂与载体；并基于作者此前关于"机器人街道家具"的研究经验进行论证。

Result: 机器人的群体中介作用并非通过模仿人类社交性实现，而是通过采取有意受限、面向群体（group-facing）的实体形态——它们为"我们"而存在和行动，却不以"我们之一"的身份卷入社会关系之中。

Conclusion: 公共空间中的机器人不应以自主性或智能来界定，而应被理解为一种关系性能力（relational capacity）。这意味着机器人的设计不应依照人的形象或为人的效用服务，而应植根于人们"共同存在与共同生成"的需求。

Abstract: Robots designed to mediate human groups often fall into the solutionist trap: they are framed as sociable agents that fix problems such as conflict, disengagement, or lack of coordination. We suggest a different way of thinking. Rather than discrete agents, robots can be understood as situated elements of shared environments; catalysts and carriers of group experience whose meaning emerges through how people position, interpret, and interact with them. From this perspective, robots are not there to repair some ostensible dysfunctionality, but to enable group-level sense-making around care, norms, and identity. Our prior work on robotic street furniture suggests that this does not happen by imitating human sociality but by taking the shape of deliberately constrained, group-facing entities that happen and act for \textit{us} without being socially entangled as one of us. We thus understand robots in public spaces not in terms of autonomy or intelligence, but as a relational capacity. This implies designing robots not in our image or for our utility, but grounded in our needs in being and becoming together.

</details>


### [321] [Distributed Stochastic Optimal Control for Pattern-Oriented Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12959)
*Qingrui Zhang,Chenghao Yu,Feng Xue,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯随机场(GRF)的随机最优控制框架，通过将GRF扩展至时间域，把群体协同建模为贝叶斯推断任务，统一解决几何控制、自组织与动态环境安全导航问题，并在仿真与实物集群实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向模式的集群在几何控制、自组织和动态环境中的安全导航方面面临多重挑战，现有方法难以在统一架构下同时处理环境不确定性、非凸约束与异构平台动力学差异。

Method: 将GRF扩展至时间域，把集群协同表述为贝叶斯推断；构建不确定性与安全性感知的避碰模块，采用无迹变换传播动态障碍物与邻近智能体的状态不确定性以获得置信边界；提出密度引导的模式控制，将几何模式编码为隐式密度场，解耦模式指定与显式智能体-目标分配，实现分布式自愈与弹性重构。

Result: 通过多场景蒙特卡洛仿真评估，在四旋翼与固定翼无人机集群上验证了模型无关性与跨异构动力学平台的泛化能力；并以15架四旋翼室内实验和4架自研自主四旋翼室外实验验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架在真实环境中能够维持可靠的几何模式转换与安全感知导航，展现出跨平台、跨场景的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: While offering significant promise for diverse applications, pattern-oriented swarms encounter multifaceted challenges in geometric control, self-organization, and safe navigation through dynamic environments. In this paper, we present a GRF-based stochastic optimal control framework to address these challenges within a unified probabilistic architecture. By extending the GRF into the temporal domain, the proposed framework casts collective coordination as a Bayesian inference task, enabling swarms to accommodate environmental uncertainty, satisfy non-convex constraints, and reconcile heterogeneous dynamics across diverse platforms. We develop an uncertainty- and safety-aware collision avoidance module for navigation in the presence of stochastic obstacle motion. The unscented transform is employed to propagate state uncertainty for both dynamic obstacles and neighboring agents, yielding principled confidence bounds for collision avoidance. In addition, density-guided pattern control is introduced, which encodes geometric patterns as implicit density fields. This representation decouples pattern specification from explicit agent-to-target assignments, thereby facilitating intrinsic self-healing and elastic reconfiguration in a distributed manner. The proposed framework is extensively evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations across diverse scenarios. Its model-agnostic nature is demonstrated on both quadrotor and fixed-wing UAV swarms, highlighting its generalizability across platforms with heterogeneous dynamics. Finally, the efficacy and robustness of the proposed method are validated through indoor experiments with a 15-quadrotor swarm and outdoor deployments involving 4 custom-built autonomous quadrotors. These experiments substantiate the proposed framework's capacity to maintain reliable geometric pattern transitions and safety-aware navigation within real-world environments.

</details>


### [322] [Tuning ROS 2 for Energy-Efficient Navigation: Empirical Insights from Costmap 2D Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12971)
*Michel Albonico,Andreas Wortmann,Ivano Malavolta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了ROS 2软件包重配置对移动机器人导航能效的影响，通过仓库场景对照实验发现配置需针对具体环境选择，并识别出影响性能与能耗的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自主导航应用日益广泛，提升能效对延长运行时间、降低环境影响至关重要。ROS作为主流中间件提供大量可配置包，但这种灵活性在动态环境中可能导致次优配置，进而损害性能并增加能耗，因此需量化重配置对能效的影响。

Method: 在两个类仓库场景（小型与大型）中开展受控实验，变化障碍物布局与Costmap 2D配置（Nav2栈的核心组件），通过重复试验测量能耗、功率曲线、CPU负载、内存消耗与导航性能。

Result: 实验表明配置必须针对具体机器人环境谨慎选择；研究识别出若干关键参数设置，它们会导致明显优良或糟糕的性能与能耗表现。

Conclusion: ROS 2包的可配置性对移动机器人导航能效具有显著影响，需根据部署环境定制参数配置，以实现性能与能耗的平衡。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly used in diverse application areas, where autonomous navigation plays a central role. As these systems become more widespread, improving their energy efficiency is critical to extending operational time and reducing environmental impact. The Robot Operating System (ROS) is a widely adopted middleware for robotics, offering a rich set of configurable packages. However, this flexibility can result in suboptimal software configurations in dynamic environments, negatively affecting both performance and energy consumption. This paper investigates the impact of ROS 2 package reconfigurations on the energy efficiency of mobile robot navigation. We conduct a controlled experiment in two warehouse-like scenarios (small and large) with varying obstacle layouts and Costmap 2D configurations (essential to the Nav2 stack). Through repeated trials, we measure energy usage, power profile, CPU load, memory consumption, and navigation performance. Results show that configurations must be carefully chosen for the specific robotic environment, and we were able to identify critical settings that lead to good and poor performance and energy consumption.

</details>


### [323] [Comfort by Construction: Adaptive, Comfort-Bounded Action Spaces for Learned Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13011)
*Anna Rothenhäusler,Daniel Jost,Raghu Rajan,Faris Janjos,Oliver Scheel,Andreas Look,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应动作参数化方法，每步按可行控制集重离散化动作网格，配合PufferDrive-Editor审计工具，在保持舒适性约束（违反率<1%）的同时提升导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的驾驶模拟器从固定动作网格生成加速度与转向率，不约束实际加速度与急动度，导致强化学习策略通过急刹急转等非人类化操作虚高安全指标，指标反映的是模拟器的宽容度而非策略质量。而朴素地施加舒适度约束也不可行：横向约束随速度二次收缩，直接裁剪静态网格会使其饱和并破坏精细控制（"网格塌缩"）。

Method: 提出自适应动作参数化：每一步都重新离散化动作网格，使其恰好覆盖该步可行的控制集合，通过横向急动度约束的闭式反演实现。同时开发PufferDrive-Editor，一个基于浏览器的工具，用于审计实际运动学并创作在运动学上具有挑战性的场景。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset和手工创作的绕桩场景上，自适应模型的舒适度违反率低于1%，同时在导航能力上优于裁剪网格基线和直接急动度基线。

Conclusion: 通过将舒适度约束内生化到动作空间中（而非事后裁剪），方法在保证真实可行性的同时维持了控制精度，为驾驶模拟器中的强化学习提供了更可信的评估基础。

Abstract: Data-driven driving simulators command accelerations and steering rates from a fixed grid without constraining the realized accelerations and jerks. As a result, reinforcement-learning policies inflate safety metrics through abrupt, last-second maneuvers that lie far outside the range of human driving and would be unacceptable to occupants of a real vehicle, so the metrics measure simulator permissiveness rather than policy quality. Enforcing comfort bounds naively is not enough: lateral limits shrink quadratically with speed, so clamping a static grid saturates it and destroys fine-grained control ("grid collapse"). We propose an adaptive action parameterization that rediscretizes the grid at every step to span exactly the per-step feasible control set, via closed-form inversion of the lateral-jerk constraint. We further present PufferDrive-Editor, a browser-based tool to audit realized kinematics and author kinematically challenging scenes. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset and a hand-authored slalom, our adaptive model holds comfort violations below 1% while outperforming clipped-grid and direct-jerk baselines in navigability.

</details>


### [324] [Global Path Planner with Multi-Model Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13015)
*Pietro Gori,Francesco Iotti,Eduard Zelenay,Rastislav Marko,Michele Pierallini,Franco Angelini,Gabriele Pannocchia,Manolo Garabini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多模型运动学切换的纯追踪控制器，通过可通行性图、Heading-Aware A*与自适应运动学建模实现跨地形的全局路径规划，并在四足机器人Artaban与四旋翼X3上验证了性能与鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同地形与机器人状态下的运动学特性差异大，单一运动学模型难以在整个路径上保持规划保真度与跟踪效率，因此需要能够根据地形特征和机器人状态实时切换模型的控制方案。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：用于地形分析的可通行性图（traversability graph）；用于生成可行路径的Heading-Aware A*算法；用于动态跟踪的多模型纯追踪控制器（multi-model Pure Pursuit）。核心为自适应运动学建模，可依据地形特征与机器人状态在多个运动学模型间实时切换。

Result: 在仿真中分别于Artaban四足机器人与X3四旋翼无人机平台进行验证，结果显示相比标准基线在性能、鲁棒性和适应性方面均有提升，并在复杂场景下优化了路径效率与能量消耗。

Conclusion: 多模型运动学切换结合纯追踪控制可有效维持跨地形的规划保真度，自适应运动学建模是提升路径效率与能效的关键，方法在不同机器人平台上具有通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work enhances global path planning via a pure-pursuit controller with multi-model kinematic switching that sustains plan fidelity across diverse terrains. The system includes a traversability graph for terrain analysis, a Heading-Aware A* algorithm for generating feasible paths, and a multi-model Pure Pursuit controller for dynamic tracking. A core innovation is adaptive kinematic modeling, enabling real-time switching between kinematic models based on terrain features and robot states. This adaptability optimizes path efficiency and energy use in challenging scenarios. We validate the approach in simulation on different platforms, namely the Artaban quadruped and the X3 quadrotor drone, showcasing improved performance, robustness, and adaptability over standard baselines.

</details>


### [325] [Dynin-Robotics: Omnimodal Unified Diffusion Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13053)
*Hoeun Lee,Jaeik Kim,Jusang Oh,Jinhyeok Kim,Geon Choi,Hyeonggeun Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Dynin-Robotics，在 omni-modal masked-diffusion 骨干 Dynin-Omni 上用共享轨迹模型统一语言条件下的动作预测、目标预测与动力学预测，并通过联合推理在多个机器人任务上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉目标预测可提供目标结果，动力学预测可提供动作依赖的场景变化表示，但二者此前未被统一纳入动作生成与选择过程。作者希望以共享轨迹模型作为共同接口，把这两类互补预测整合进语言条件的机器人策略中。

Method: 将语言、视觉观测、目标与动作统一表示为离散 token，在 omni-modal masked-diffusion 骨干 Dynin-Omni 上建模。通过改变条件跨度与目标跨度，同一模型可学习动作预测、动作条件下下一观测预测、终端目标状态预测以及轨迹到指令的重建。该接口支持测试时扩展，包括目标预测、动作候选评估以及动作与未来状态预测的联合精修。模型在 48 个 Open X-Embodiment 数据集约 133 万条轨迹上继续预训练，再分别适配下游领域。

Result: 在 VLABench 的两个任务中，机器人预训练在固定 Stage-2 步数预算下提升适配效果；完整目标组合在同一耦合解码器下相比仅策略后训练提高了偏移指令成功率。目标引导结合动作-下一状态联合去噪进一步优于仅动作解码，收益取决于预测组合方式。在 LIBERO 和零样本 LIBERO-Plus 上性能有竞争力，在 Franka Research 3 上四种操作条件平均成功率达 78.4%。优化后的块并行实现使模型侧动作解码相对基础实现最高加速 29.2 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明共享轨迹建模可作为学习互补机器人目标并在控制中组合其预测的通用接口。

Abstract: Visual goal and dynamics prediction can provide language-conditioned robot policies with both a target outcome and a representation of action-dependent scene changes. We bring these predictions into action generation and selection through a shared trajectory model. Dynin-Robotics implements this formulation on Dynin-Omni, an omnimodal masked-diffusion backbone, representing language, visual observations, goals, and actions as discrete tokens. By varying conditioning and target spans, the same model learns action prediction, action-conditioned next-observation prediction, terminal goal-state prediction, and trajectory-to-instruction reconstruction. These interfaces support test-time scaling through goal prediction, action-candidate evaluation, and joint refinement of action and future-state predictions. We continually pretrain the model on approximately 1.33 million trajectories from 48 Open X-Embodiment datasets and adapt it separately to downstream domains. On two VLABench tasks, robot pretraining improves adaptation within a fixed Stage-2 step budget, and the full objective mixture improves shifted-instruction success over Policy-only post-training under the same coupled decoder. Combining goal guidance with joint action-next-state denoising further improves shifted-instruction success over action-only decoding; the benefit depends on how the predictions are composed. Dynin-Robotics achieves competitive performance on LIBERO and zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, together with a 78.4% average success rate across four manipulation conditions on a Franka Research 3 robot. An optimized block-parallel implementation accelerates model-side action decoding by up to 29.2x relative to the base implementation under the reported profiling setup. These results support shared trajectory modeling as a common interface for learning complementary robot objectives and composing their predictions during control.

</details>


### [326] [ASTRIL-MPC: Autonomous Traversal Framework of Articulated Tracked Robots with Language-Guided Neural-Kinematic MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13083)
*Zhenfeng Gan,Yanbo Chen,Lirong Che,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASTRIL-MPC 将学习运动学模型、NMPC 与 LLM 引导的参数重调相结合，实现关节履带机器人在楼梯与杂乱室内环境中的自主通行，通行质量分数最高提升 71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市搜救中关节履带机器人需通过楼梯井与杂乱室内等结构化但接触密集的环境。其自主性难点在于机器人-地形交互（RTI）具有混合、非连续特性，且履带-摆臂协调难以解析建模。

Method: 提出 ASTRIL-MPC：以语言引导的神经运动学 MPC 框架。学习型运动学模型由高度序列与近期轨迹预测短时域任务状态增量；NMPC 采用多目标代价与严格可行性约束进行规划；LLM 通过带范围裁剪、速率限制与一致性检查的安全接口，对选定权重与边界提出有界更新。编译后的预测器使完整控制周期在 100 ms 内完成。

Result: 在三项通行任务与多高度泛化设定下，ASTRIL-MPC 的聚合通行质量分数较非自适应 NMPC 最高提升 71%，较 PPO 基线提升 67%，并在下降过程中消除可测的碰撞冲击。

Conclusion: 结合学习运动学、基于优化的规划与语言引导的重新调参，可为关节履带机器人带来数据高效且鲁棒的自主能力。

Abstract: In urban search and rescue, articulated tracked robots (ATRs) must traverse structured but contact-rich environments such as stairwells and cluttered building interiors. Reliable autonomy remains challenging because robot-terrain interaction (RTI) is hybrid and discontinuous, and effective flipper-track coordination is difficult to model analytically. We present ASTRIL-MPC, a language-guided neural kinematics model predictive control (MPC) framework for autonomous traversal. A learned kinematics model predicts short-horizon task-state increments from a height sequence and recent trajectories; NMPC plans with multi-objective costs and strict feasibility constraints; and a large language model (LLM) proposes bounded updates to selected weights and bounds through a safety-checked interface with range clipping, rate limiting, and consistency checks. The compiled predictor enables a full control cycle within 100 ms. Across three traversal tasks and a multi-height generalization setting, ASTRIL-MPC improves an aggregate traversal-quality score by up to 71% over a non-adaptive NMPC and by 67% over a PPO baseline, while eliminating measurable collision impacts during descent. These results indicate that combining learned kinematics, optimization-based planning, and language-guided retuning yields data-efficient and robust autonomy for articulated tracked robots.

</details>


### [327] [ARC: Autonomous Robotics Compliance A Three-Layer Governance Architecture for Deployed Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12932)
*Tord Eide,Einar Holt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向自主机器人系统的治理框架，核心为三层合规架构（ARC），由模型安全验证、认知认证基准与运行授权标准三个层次构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人系统能力快速提升，但其安全性、认知可靠性与部署授权缺乏统一、可操作的治理与合规路径，亟需构建分层治理框架以衔接技术验证与制度监管。

Method: 提出三层合规架构（ARC）：一是模型安全验证，针对模型层面的安全属性进行检验；二是认知认证基准，用以评估系统的认知能力与可靠性；三是运行授权标准，规范实际部署与运行的准入条件。

Result: 构建了一个由模型安全验证、认知认证基准与运行授权标准逐层递进组成的ARC架构，为自主机器人系统的合规评估提供了结构化、模块化的框架。

Conclusion: 该ARC框架将技术验证与治理制度相衔接，可为自主机器人系统的安全性保障、认证评估与部署授权提供统一的合规路径；后续需在具体场景中验证其可操作性与适用性。

Abstract: Proposed governance framework for autonomous robotic systems, introducing a three-layer compliance architecture (ARC) instantiated through model safety validation, cognitive certification benchmarks, and operational authorization standards.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [328] [The Computational Primitives of Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11989)
*Jonathan W. Page*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出"计算原语理论"（CPT），主张适应性系统存在一套共同的、与物理载体无关的计算结构，包括Arouse、Orient、Valence、Position、Boundary、Attune六个原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统适应性系统研究聚焦于行为层（有机体做什么）与机制层（其机制如何运作），而忽视了第三层——计算层（适应性系统为生存与繁殖必须计算什么）。作者旨在填补这一空缺，为生物与人工系统提供统一的适应性计算框架。

Method: 通过四条标准筛选原语：存在必要性、跨独立演化谱系的普遍性、演化保守性、不可还原性，最终确定六个原语：Arouse、Orient、Valence、Position、Boundary、Attune。并由此推导出对生物系统与人工系统的两方面启示。

Result: 在生物系统方面，原语提供了一种载体中立的方法用于跨物种比较，将注意、记忆、决策等精细化行为重新表述为这些计算的组合。在人工系统方面，原语为当前机器智能的诸多挑战（虚构、提示注入、易分心、奖励劫持、灾难性遗忘）提供了新视角，暗示这些问题可能源于相应计算的缺失。

Conclusion: 对于能够持续存在的生物与人工系统而言，并不需要特定的物理载体，而必须实现这些计算原语才能具备适应性。生物学对机器智能的启示不在于大脑的神经设计，而在于其演化出的计算功能。CPT目前是一个工作假设，有待通过讨论、实证检验与应用进一步完善。

Abstract: Research on adaptive systems has traditionally focused on behavior (what organisms do) and mechanism (how their machinery works). This paper focuses on a third level, computation, which considers what adaptive systems must compute to survive and reproduce. It is proposed that adaptation has its own computational structure, comprising a small set of primitive operations common to all adaptive systems, regardless of their physical form. Six primitives, Arouse, Orient, Valence, Position, Boundary, and Attune, were selected using four criteria: necessity for existence, universality across independently evolved lineages, evolutionary conservation, and irreducibility. From this, two main implications follow. First, in biological systems, the primitives provide a substrate-neutral method for cross-species comparisons, reframing elaborative behaviors like attention, memory, and decision-making as combinations of these computations. Second, in artificial systems, the primitives offer a new way to view current challenges in machine intelligence, such as confabulation, prompt injection, distractibility, reward hacking, and catastrophic forgetting, suggesting that these issues may arise from a lack of these computations. Thus, for biological and artificial systems that persist, no specific physical substrate is necessary; rather, these primitives must be implemented if the systems are to be adaptive. What biology offers to inform machine intelligence, then, is not the brain's neural design but the computational functions it evolved to perform. The Computational Primitives Theory (CPT) presented here is a working hypothesis, intended for further refinement through discussion, empirical testing, and application.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [329] [Asynchronous Parallel Search for Exact Multi-Objective Shortest Paths with Versioned Frontier Snapshots and Indexed Dominance Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11944)
*Xiaoqing Xu,Ning Zhang,Liuyihui Qian,Xiaojun Liu,Juan Wu,Hong Tang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SIP-MOSP异步精确多目标最短路框架，通过版本化前沿快照与索引剪枝解耦标签扩展与前沿维护，实现高效并行，最高加速46.9倍并大幅降低内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确MOSP计算完整Pareto集时，非支配标签集膨胀和逐顶点Pareto前沿上的频繁支配测试导致计算代价急剧上升，且现有方法难以高效并行化。

Method: SIP-MOSP在单一协同搜索中分离标签扩展与前沿维护，结合不可变版本化前沿快照与索引化支配剪枝，避免对同一可变前沿的并发访问；实例化为块最小值索引（SIP-MOSP-BM）与线段树最小值索引（SIP-MOSP-ST）两种变体，并证明其精确性。

Result: 在道路网络、ISP拓扑与180顶点完全有向图上评估，相较最优顺序基线最高加速46.9倍，相较最优并行基线最高加速7.05倍；20目标完全图场景下，SIP-MOSP-ST相对最优并行基线加速3.34倍，峰值内存降低60.3倍。

Conclusion: SIP-MOSP是一个适用于结构多样图拓扑的高效共享内存精确MOSP框架。

Abstract: Exact multi-objective shortest-path (MOSP) search computes the complete Pareto set between specified start and goal vertices, and its computational cost can grow rapidly with expanding nondominated label sets and frequent dominance tests over per-vertex Pareto frontiers. Efficiently parallelizing exact MOSP remains an open challenge. This paper presents SIP-MOSP (Snapshot-based Indexed-Pruning MOSP), an asynchronous exact framework that separates label expansion from frontier maintenance within a single cooperative search. SIP-MOSP combines immutable versioned frontier snapshots with indexed dominance pruning, enabling concurrent label processing without concurrent access to the same mutable frontier. Together, these mechanisms reduce synchronization overhead and accelerate dominance testing. We instantiate the framework with block-minimum (SIP-MOSP-BM) and segment-tree-minimum (SIP-MOSP-ST) indices and prove exactness. We evaluate both variants against four state-of-the-art exact MOSP baselines covering sequential and parallel search. Experiments across multiple objective dimensions on a road network, an Internet service provider topology, and an 180-vertex complete directed graph show that SIP-MOSP achieves speedups of up to 46.9* over the best-performing sequential baseline and up to 7.05* over the best-performing parallel baseline on mutually solved instances. In the 20-objective complete-graph setting, where many instances remain unsolved by the sequential baselines within one hour, SIP-MOSP-ST achieves a 3.34* speedup while reducing peak memory by a factor of 60.3 relative to the best-performing parallel baseline. These results demonstrate that SIP-MOSP is an efficient shared-memory framework for exact MOSP across structurally diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [330] [Efficient Vision-Language-Action Management and Serving for Robot Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12075)
*Dionysios Adamopoulos,Nattapol Chanpaisit,Basel Fakhri,Christina Giannoula*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Robion是首个面向多机器人、多模型请求的多GPU边缘VLA服务与管理系统，通过GPU内两流阶段解耦、SM动态限制、跨模型流共享及智能流量控制，在满足SLO的同时显著提升服务负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型推理为延迟关键型任务，需满足严格的SLO，但高端GPU无法直接部署于机器人端；现有边缘服务系统面向吞吐优化且按阶段跨GPU解耦，不适配VLA毫秒级多阶段特性，缺乏多请求、多模型、多GPU场景下的SLO支持。

Method: 采用两阶段设计（VLM+ADiT）。服务引擎在单GPU内用两条流解耦VLM与ADiT阶段，动态限制VLM流的SM占用以保证ADiT可并行调度，并通过跨模型流共享实现多模型共置，按最少剩余SLO时间优先调度请求；管理引擎支持多GPU灵活模型放置，并集成智能流量控制器，在选定放置下最大化每模型批处理，同时限制每GPU负载以达成SLO。

Result: 单模型场景下，Robion在98% SLO达成率内支持的平均机器人负载分别比vLLM-Omni和Monolithic高6.7倍和1.5倍；在4-GPU服务器上服务8个不同模型的大规模实验中，可支持多达64个机器人达到98% SLO达成率。

Conclusion: Robion通过GPU内阶段解耦、流共享与流量控制的协同设计，有效解决了多机器人、多模型VLA服务在边缘多GPU环境下的SLO保障问题，显著优于现有服务系统。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show high robotic manipulation capabilities via a two-stage design: a Vision-Language Model (VLM) stage followed by an Action Diffusion Transformer (ADiT) stage. Since robots must meet strict Service-Level Objectives (SLOs) for safety, VLA inference is inherently latency-critical. Meeting these SLOs requires high-end GPUs, yet weight, cost, and power constraints preclude integrating such GPUs on-robot. Prior works offload VLA inference to edge servers that serve many robots on VLA models. However, current VLA systems lack support for multi-request, multi-model execution on a multi-GPU server under SLOs, while existing serving systems for multi-stage models are optimized for throughput and stage disaggregation across separate GPUs, which are ill-suited for the millisecond-scale stages of VLA models. We design Robion, the first VLA serving and management system for multi-robot, multi-model requests on multi-GPU edge servers that meets SLOs. Our serving engine disaggregates the VLM and ADiT stages within a GPU via two streams, dynamically restricting the SMs on VLM stream so ADiT always finds SMs to run alongside it, and co-locates multiple models by sharing these streams across them, prioritizing requests by least remaining SLO time. Our management engine enables flexible model placements on multi-GPU servers, and integrates an intelligent traffic controller that maximizes per-model batching under the chosen placement while bounding each GPU's load to meet SLOs. For individual models, Robion serves on average 6.7$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ higher robot load within 98% SLO attainment over vLLM-Omni, the most widely used multi-stage serving system, and Monolithic, which runs VLM and ADiT as a single pipeline, respectively. In a large-scale experiment of serving 8 different models on a 4-GPU server, Robion can serve up to 64 robots within 98% SLO attainment.

</details>


### [331] [Consensus-based Decentralized Distributed Swarm Learning with Heterogeneous Big Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12143)
*Zhuoyu Yao,Dong Yang,Yue Wang,Songyang Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zhi Tian,Zhipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CD-DSL框架，将共识优化与粒子群优化结合，用于无线边缘网络去中心化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能面临非凸目标、数据异构和复杂无线网络拓扑等挑战。

Method: 融合共识优化与PSO：邻域模型共识协调、PSO启发式更新利用历史与邻居经验，并提出自适应邻居混合策略学习性能感知的共识权重。

Result: 理论证明保持参与者一致性并在非凸目标下实现非遍历收敛至稳定点邻域；实验表明可缓解数据异构导致的现有去中心化基线性能退化。

Conclusion: CD-DSL有效提升异构边缘设备去中心化协作的鲁棒性与收敛性能。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence increasingly relies on large-scale, distributed, and heterogeneous data collected by edge devices. However, the practice of edge intelligence remains challenging due to non-convex objectives, data heterogeneity, and complex wireless network topology. To address these issues, this paper proposes a consensus-based decentralized distributed swarm learning (CD-DSL) framework for wireless edge networks. Our CD-DSL integrates consensus optimization with particle swarm optimization (PSO), by reaching the model consensus among neighboring devices while leveraging the PSO exploration and exploitation. The consensus mechanism supports decentralized coordination without raw-data exchange, while PSO-inspired updates utilize historical and neighbor-shared experience to enhance exploration for non-convex optimization, improve robustness to data heterogeneity, and accelerate convergence. We further develop an adaptive neighbor-mixing strategy that learns performance-aware consensus weights, improving decentralized collaboration among heterogeneous edge devices. Theoretical analysis establishes that CD-DSL maintains participant consistency and achieves non-ergodic convergence to a neighborhood of a stationary point under non-convex objectives. Experimental results show that CD-DSL can mitigate the performance degeneration of existing decentralized baselines caused by heterogeneous data.

</details>


### [332] [Specifying Paxos for System Builders: Pseudocode Made Executable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12239)
*Yanhong A. Liu,Rahul Sihag*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文用DistAlgo将Paxos for System Builders伪代码逐行映射为可精确执行的形式化规范，用于理解、验证和可视化协议运行，并借此发现并修复了伪代码中的小遗漏与活性bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Paxos for System Builders是分布式系统复制与共识的实用协议，已有C实现和伪代码，但伪代码难以直接执行与自动检查，需要精确、可执行的规范来帮助理解协议逻辑并进行验证、追踪和可视化。

Method: 采用高层语言DistAlgo，将协议伪代码基本逐行地表达为可精确执行的形式化规范，从而可直接在分布式系统中运行，便于自动检查、追踪与可视化协议运行过程。

Result: 得到的程序规模与伪代码相近，性能可接受；且在转化与执行过程中发现并修复了伪代码（而非C代码）中微小、难以察觉的遗漏和活性bug。

Conclusion: 精确规范与直接执行显著有助于理解协议逻辑及自动检查、追踪、可视化协议运行，并能揭示伪代码中难以发现的缺陷，同时保持与伪代码相当的规模与可接受的性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a precise executable specification---as a faithful mapping from the pseudocode---of Paxos for System Builders, a practical protocol for replication and consensus in distributed systems. Paxos for System Builders has both a robust implementation in C and a clean pseudocode for critical protocol details.
  This paper shows how the protocol pseudocode can be expressed easily, essentially line-by-line, in a precise high-level language, DistAlgo, for direct execution in distributed systems. Precise specification and direct execution help significantly in understanding the protocol logic and in automatically checking, tracing, and visualizing protocol runs. They also led to discoveries and fixes of small, difficult-to-catch omissions and liveness bugs in the pseudocode though not the C code. The resulting program also has acceptable performance while having similar size as the pseudocode.

</details>


### [333] [Argus: Orchestrating Cross-Layer GPU Performance Measurements around Semantic Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12299)
*Jianzhu Yao,Yue Guan,Srivatsan Ramesh,Yuanwei Fang,Jian Jiao,Boda Li,Yueming Hao,Xinwei Qiang,Pramod Viswanath,Yufei Ding,Bill Yoshimi,Alexey Loginov,Shane Nay,Adnan Aziz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Argus 是一个以语义区域为中心的测量规划与运行时系统，自动化 GPU 代码区域的性能证据收集与归因，在算子优化、持久化 megakernel 及跨层级 PGO 场景中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 开发者与自动优化器需要针对语义区域（如神经网络算子实现、流水线阶段）的性能证据，但此类证据分散于各类性能分析工具中。回答区域级问题往往需人工构建探针与程序变体、隔离干扰性测量，并将证据映射到区域和执行上下文，流程繁琐。

Method: Argus 采用区域标记（boundary markers）识别语义区域，由客户端选择信号与执行范围。系统在编译、执行和测量变体间保持区域身份一致性，构建干扰感知的多运行测量计划，并跨多种后端编排变换与性能分析。最终利用区域身份与动态执行上下文，联合编译器级、硬件级与系统级证据，生成记录测量来源与归因歧义的报告。

Result: 在智能体算子优化场景中，44 个 persistent-GEMM 与注意力配置有 39/44 得到改进，将 AlphaEvolve 的几何平均加速比从 5.4% 提升至 8.9%；在 persistent TinyLlama-1.1B 解码 megakernel 上，优化智能体达到 1.65 ms/token（无 Argus 时为 4.92 ms/token），内核比使用 CUDA Graphs 的 PyTorch 快 2.1 倍；在跨层级 PGO 中改善计算-通信重叠，五个多 GPU 设置平均吞吐提升 7%。

Conclusion: Argus 通过区域为中心的自动化测量规划，解决了跨工具性能证据碎片化问题，在算子优化、持久化 megakernel 与跨层级 PGO 三类场景中均取得一致且显著的性能收益，验证了其通用性与有效性。

Abstract: GPU developers and automated optimizers need performance evidence for semantic code regions--such as neural-network operator implementations and pipeline stages--but this evidence is fragmented across profiling tools. Answering a region-level question can require manually constructing probes and program variants, isolating interfering measurements, and mapping evidence to regions and execution contexts. We present Argus, a region-centric measurement planner and runtime that automates this workflow. Clients identify regions with boundary markers and select signals and execution scopes. Argus preserves region identity across compilation, execution, and measurement variants, constructs interference-aware multi-run plans, and orchestrates transformations and profiling across backends. It joins compiler-, hardware-, and system-level evidence using region identity and dynamic execution context, producing reports that record measurement origins and attribution ambiguity.
  We evaluate Argus across agentic kernel optimization, persistent megakernel optimization, and cross-level PGO. Across 44 persistent-GEMM and attention configurations, Argus improves 39/44 cases and raises AlphaEvolve's geometric-mean speedup from 5.4% to 8.9%. On a persistent TinyLlama-1.1B decode megakernel, an optimization agent reaches 1.65 ms/token with Argus versus 4.92 ms/token without it, producing a kernel $2.1\times$ faster than PyTorch with CUDA Graphs. Finally, Argus-guided cross-level PGO improves compute--communication overlap, increasing throughput by 7% on average across five multi-GPU settings.

</details>


### [334] [GreenDirector: carbon- and water-aware workload placement for sustainable computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12602)
*Jime Iglesias Blanco,Ignacio Heredia,María Castrillo,Andrei Tsaregorodtsev,Mazen Ezzeddine,Álvaro López García*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该文提出Environmental Score（ES）与Green Score（GS）两个无量纲指标，将碳足迹与时空、稀缺度加权的水影响统一纳入计算调度决策，并在AI4EOSC与DIRAC两个生产级联邦基础设施中验证其可行性与减碳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心电力需求因AI加速增长，仅以碳排放衡量计算的环境影响并不完整：低碳电力结构往往耗水密集，且水危害取决于当地与季节性的稀缺程度而非用水总量。现有调度与核算缺乏能同时表达碳与水的统一度量。

Method: 提出ES指标，取值[0,100]，结合实时跨境电力流溯源与月度AWARE2.0水稀缺特征因子，将全球温室气体排放与本地、季节性水压力严重程度加权统一。在此基础上定义GS，即单位真实环境影响所交付有用计算工作量，并计入数据中心功率与硬件效率。二者作为绿色亲和特征接入AI4EOSC与DIRAC的GreenDirector调度器。

Result: AI4EOSC中跨四个泛欧提供商的集群填充实验表明，更绿色的站点被优先填充，且调度时延与终端用户体验未退化。DIRAC中133,631个作业的轨迹驱动仿真及KM3NeT社区的初步生产部署，使碳排放降低、碳效率提升约40%，并在最低碳站点同时水压力更高时，显式呈现碳-水权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，水文压力可被动态加权进真实多租户系统中的工作负载放置决策，碳-水联合核算与调度具备生产可行性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of data center electricity demand, accelerated by AI, makes carbon-only accounting an incomplete measure of computing's environmental impact: low-carbon electricity mixes are often water-intensive, and the resulting harm depends on local, seasonal scarcity rather than on the volume of water consumed. We propose the Environmental Score (ES), a unified, dimensionless index in $[0, 100]$ that jointly captures the carbon footprint and the spatial-temporal, scarcity-weighted water impact of the electricity a workload consumes. It combines real-time, cross-border electricity flow tracing with monthly AWARE2.0 water-scarcity characterization factors, weighting global greenhouse-gas emissions together with the local, seasonal severity of water stress. Building on it, we define the Green Score (GS), a scheduling metric proportional to the useful computational work delivered per unit of real environmental impact, which also accounts for data center power and hardware efficiency. We add both metrics as a green-affinity feature to the GreenDirector schedulers of two production federated infrastructures, the AI4EOSC scientific cloud and the DIRAC workload management system. In AI4EOSC, a cluster-filling experiment over four pan-European providers shows that greener sites are filled first without degrading scheduling latency or end-user experience. In DIRAC, a trace-driven simulation of 133,631 jobs and a preliminary production deployment for the KM3NeT community reduce carbon emissions and improve carbon efficiency by about 40\%, while making the carbon-water trade-off explicit when the lowest-carbon site also carries higher water stress. The results show that hydrological stress can be dynamically weighted into workload placement in live, multi-tenant systems

</details>


### [335] [A Dynamic Vertical Scaling Strategy for Distributed Stream Processing Applications in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12975)
*Guilherme Hiago Costa dos Santos,Carlos Henrique Kayser,Tiago Coelho Ferreto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出基于PPO的动态垂直扩展策略，将边端分布式流处理的任务分配与延迟SLO合规建模为POMDP，在EdgeStreamPy仿真中以0.03%-0.30%的违规率优于VRebalance且CPU占用更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘环境下的分布式流处理需在有限且异构的资源上同时兼顾低延迟与高吞吐，资源约束与工作负载波动使静态或粗粒度扩展难以维持p95端到端延迟的SLO合规，需要自适应的垂直扩展决策机制。

Method: 将动态垂直扩展策略建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），采用近端策略优化（PPO）学习策略；策略动作联合调整任务分配，并以p95端到端延迟SLO合规为优先优化目标；在EdgeStreamPy仿真平台开展实验，覆盖两种应用配置、两种工作负载，每种组合含十组配对放置，对比不同决策频率的完整控制器配置。

Result: 所选策略保持了吞吐量，平均违规率介于0.03%至0.30%，在所有场景中均低于VRebalance基线；在四组组合中的三组使用了更少CPU。对比覆盖了不同决策频率的完整控制器配置。

Conclusion: 基于PPO的POMDP垂直扩展策略能在边端流处理中有效兼顾吞吐与p95延迟SLO合规，相较VRebalance在违规率与CPU效率上更具优势，为资源受限异构环境下动态资源分配与SLO保障提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Distributed Stream Processing applications at the edge must reconcile low latency and high throughput with limited and heterogeneous resources. This paper presents a dynamic vertical scaling strategy based on Proximal Policy Optimization, formulated as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process. The policy jointly adjusts task allocations and prioritizes compliance with a p95 end-to-end latency Service Level Objective. In EdgeStreamPy simulation experiments with two application profiles, two workloads, and ten paired placements per combination, the selected policies preserved throughput, obtained mean violation rates from 0.03% to 0.30%, below VRebalance in every scenario, and used less CPU in three of four combinations. The comparison covers complete controller configurations with different decision frequencies.

</details>


### [336] [RoofLang: Enabling AI-Driven Architecting of LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12551)
*Ziyue Yang,Yuting Jiang,Lei Qu,Peng Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出RoofLang领域特定语言，通过通用工作负载表示、可验证变异空间与实现无关评估器，使AI能突破基于profiling的搜索局限，实现LLM推理系统的架构级设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI优化LLM推理主要依赖profiling反馈，这种反馈将搜索空间限制在既有软件栈的能力与性能范围内，无法发现本质上更优的LLM推理系统架构。

Method: 提出RoofLang DSL，提供三项关键能力：通用工作负载表示、可验证变异空间、实现无关的评估器，以支撑AI驱动的LLM推理系统架构设计循环。

Result: RoofLang揭示DeepSeek V4系列模型可实现比其他代表性模型高3.5–39.5倍的峰值解码吞吐，该差距与总参数量不成比例，主要源于支持更大批次并降低内存流量的紧凑KV-cache设计；持续性优化器agent进一步发现了若干新架构，在NVIDIA B300上将DeepSeek V4 Pro的吞吐与交互性提升6.23–50.1%。

Conclusion: 通过通用工作负载表示、可验证变异空间与实现无关评估器，RoofLang使AI能够突破profiling限制进行推理系统架构搜索，验证了架构级优化在吞吐与交互性上的显著潜力。

Abstract: AI is beginning to make substantive contributions to LLM inference optimization. Existing AI optimizations are predominantly profiling-based. Profiling-bound feedback confines the search to the capabilities and performance of an existing software stack, preventing a fundamentally better architecture of LLM inference systems from being identified. To enable the AI-driven LLM inference system architecting loop, we argue that a general workload representation, a verifiable mutation space, and an implementation-independent evaluator are required. We present the RoofLang domain-specific language (DSL) that provides these features. In our evaluation, RoofLang reveals that DeepSeek V4-series models could achieve 3.5-39.5$\times$ higher peak decode throughput than other representative models. This gap is disproportionate to their total parameter counts and arises largely from compact KV-cache designs that support larger batches and reduce memory traffic. A persistent optimizer agent further discovered several new architectures that improved both throughput and interactivity of DeepSeek V4 Pro on NVIDIA B300 by 6.23-50.1%.

</details>


### [337] [HoliBench: A Cross-Platform Benchmarking and Deployment Toolkit for Foundation Models in CPS-IoT Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12412)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,Zejun Xiong,Pragya Sharma,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HoliBench：面向基础模型部署的模块化基准测试与部署工具包，联合刻画跨平台精度、延迟与能耗，揭示现有工具遗漏的权衡，并证明单模型画像可按顺序共驻执行组合预测多模型流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（LLM、视觉语言模型、时序基础模型）日益部署于嵌入式与边缘平台（CPS/IoT），能耗、延迟、内存与任务精度同等关键。但现有基准工具只孤立评估模型能力（假设算力充足仅报精度），硬件剖析工具则平台特定且互不兼容，导致用户缺乏跨异构设备做部署决策的统一工作流。

Method: 提出 HoliBench：模块化基准测试与部署工具包，联合刻画从单板计算机到 GPU 服务器的精度、延迟与能耗。其平台抽象层校准跨设备测量；支持多模型模态、推理引擎、并发度及现有评估框架；交互界面基于"一次画像、跨研究复用"的设计空间提供约束感知的配置选择。

Result: 在 20 个模型、7 类设备、3 种量化等级、8 个推理后端、30+ 任务上完成刻画，揭示现有工具遗漏的权衡：量化仅在具备低精度支持的硬件上降低延迟；精度提升相对能耗呈递减收益；自回归工作负载的平均推理功率在输出长度上近似恒定。单模型画像可在顺序共驻执行下组合——多模型 CPS 部署中，独立画像对组合流水线延迟和功率的预测误差分别为 1.2% 和 2.5%，无需穷举每套流水线配置。

Conclusion: HoliBench 作为开源基础设施，支持面向部署的基础模型评估，实现无需穷举配置的多模型流水线部署探索。

Abstract: Foundation models, including large language models, vision-language models, and time-series foundation models, are increasingly deployed on embedded and edge platforms for CPS and IoT applications, where energy, latency, and memory are as critical as task accuracy. Existing benchmarking tools evaluate model capability in isolation, reporting accuracy assuming sufficient compute, while hardware profiling tools remain platform-specific and mutually incompatible. As a result, users lack a unified workflow for making deployment decisions across heterogeneous devices. We present HoliBench, a modular benchmarking and deployment toolkit that jointly characterizes accuracy, latency, and energy across platforms from single-board computers to GPU servers. Its platform abstraction layer calibrates cross-device measurement, and the toolkit supports multiple model modalities, inference engines, concurrencies, and existing evaluation harnesses. An interactive interface exposes constraint-aware configuration selection over a design space that is profiled once and reused across studies. Using HoliBench, we characterize 20 models across 7 device types, 3 quantization levels, 8 inference backends, and over 30 tasks, surfacing tradeoffs that existing tools miss: quantization reduces latency only on hardware with low-precision support, accuracy gains show diminishing returns relative to energy, and for autoregressive workloads, average inference power is approximately constant across output lengths. We further find that single-model profiles compose under sequential co-resident execution. In a multi-model CPS deployment, standalone profiles predict combined-pipeline latency and power within 1.2% and 2.5%, enabling deployment exploration without exhaustively profiling every pipeline configuration. We release HoliBench as open-source infrastructure for deployment-aware evaluation of foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [338] [A Survey on Quantum-Safe Cryptographic Mechanisms: Building Blocks and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11991)
*Ricardo Parizotto,Preeti Yadav,Marcus Freire,Anderson Tomkelski,Maycon Peixoto,Emmanuel Zambrini Cruzeiro,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文综述抗量子密码机制的技术现状、现有迁移应用及开放挑战，并对安全构件与应用领域进行分类梳理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机的可能出现会威胁现有公钥加密与数字签名算法，危及现有基础设施安全，因此亟需研发能抵御量子计算风险的安全机制。然而向新机制的迁移仍在进行中，面临识别受威胁应用、实现敏捷可扩展迁移流程等挑战。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理抗量子（quantum-safe）密码机制的最新进展；考察面向迁移的现有应用；分析常用于将抗量子机制集成到现有应用中的构件，并按所采用的安全机制对其进行分类；系统回顾已有的抗量子方案迁移工作，并按应用领域（电信、物联网、区块链）进行分类。

Result: 形成了抗量子安全构件按安全机制的分类体系，以及现有迁移工作按应用领域（电信、物联网、区块链）的类别划分，并归纳了成功迁移所需应对的挑战及迁移动机。

Conclusion: 向抗量子机制的迁移是必要且紧迫的，但仍需解决诸多挑战；本文为理解抗量子密码的技术构件、迁移实践与开放问题提供了系统性的综述框架。

Abstract: The possible emergence of fault-tolerant quantum computers may enable widely used cryptographic algorithms to be broken. These algorithms for public key encryption and digital signatures could be exposed if an efficient algorithm is employed. This poses a threat to existing infrastructure, motivating the development of mechanisms that can withstand quantum-computer cybersecurity risks. However, the transition to new security mechanisms is still underway and faces many challenges, including identifying which applications are under threat and implementing agile, scalable migration processes. In this work, we survey the state of the art in quantum-safe cryptographic mechanisms, existing applications targeting migration, and open challenges. We examine the building blocks commonly used to integrate
  quantum-safe mechanisms into existing applications and categorize them by the security mechanisms they employ. Next, we systematically review existing migrations into quantum-safe schemes and categorize them by application domain, including Telecommunications, the Internet of Things, and Blockchains. Finally, we summarize the challenges that must be addressed to enable a successful migration to quantum-resistant mechanisms, as well as the motivations for pursuing this migration.

</details>


### [339] [A Dark Forest First Attack Is Rational Only If the Attacker Accepts It as Its Last](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12003)
*Harvey Dam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重构"黑暗森林"论证：将敌方打击对象界定为"未被控制、可预警或报复的剩余能力"，且攻击者永不知名册是否完整。首次打击只有在"清除所获时机收益≥所有幸存者知悉带来的暴露损失"时才理性；由于未知者构成不可清除的幸存者，先发打击等同于最后一次攻击，仅当等待会使清除成本上升约等于全部赌注时才理性。发送信号使接收方获得对发送者能力的后验而非目标，并证明发送者可预警，故信标可威慑：发送者无需能打击任何人，只需能告知能做到的人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有黑暗森林模型把被探测文明设为打击对象，并在攻击者已知的名册上进行打击；其主张克制的建议所依赖的步骤恰在其适用情形中失效。本文旨在修正这一对象设定与知识假设，重构先发打击与信号传输的理性条件。

Method: 形式化建模：以"未被控制的预警或报复能力"为敌意对象，名册不可完备；比较首次打击的时机收益与幸存者知悉的暴露损失。进一步对信号传输建模：发送即给出接收方关于发送者能力的后验，并证明发送者可预警；区分接收方是否敌意，分析信标对敌意接收方的延迟作用及"免费信标"造成损害的四种途径。

Result: (1) 先发打击仅在等待会使清除目标的成本上升约等于攻击者全部赌注时才理性，且先发者等于放弃未来一切攻击。(2) 能造成攻击者毁灭的幸存者（直接打击或警告有能力者）使攻击者损失全部赌注，而未知者即此类不可清除的幸存者。(3) 发送信号给出的是接收方对发送者能力的后验而非目标，并证明发送者可预警。(4) 免费信标仅在"确定击杀""全赌注紧迫性""接收方不惧发送者可警告的任何对象""发送者未知的入口"四种情形下损害发送者。(5) 对敌意接收方，信标延迟所有无法几乎确定清除发送者力量的敌意者。

Conclusion: METI应以"可达范围"和"接收方必须相信什么才会行动"来评判，而非惩罚恐惧；信标通过"见证"发挥威慑——发送者不必能打击任何人，只要能告诉能做到的人。因此黑暗森林式先发打击的理性空间远小于既有模型，而信号传输的威慑逻辑应被重新定位。

Abstract: The Dark Forest argument holds that a civilization that detects another should strike it at once, so detection, and any transmission that invites it, is danger. Existing models make the detected civilization the object of the strike and play the strike on a roster the attacker knows; where they advise restraint, the advice rests on steps that fail in the cases it is for. This paper takes the object of hostility to be remaining uncontrolled capacity to warn or retaliate, on a roster no attacker ever knows to be complete. A first strike is rational only if the timing benefit of what it removes is at least the disclosure loss from every leftover that learns of it. A leftover able to bring about the attacker's destruction, by striking or by warning one who can, costs the attacker its whole stake, and the actors the attacker has never found are such a leftover that no strike removes. A Dark Forest believer that strikes first therefore accepts the strike as the last attack it will ever make; the strike is rational only if waiting would raise the cost of removing the target by about the attacker's whole stake. A transmission that reveals the sender gives a receiver a posterior over the sender's capacity, not a target, and proves that the sender can warn. A receiver need not be hostile; for one that is, the sender is a witness it must pay for, so a beacon delays every hostile that cannot be nearly sure of removing the sender's forces, and a free beacon harms its sender only through sure kill, whole-stake urgency, a receiver that fears no one the sender could warn, or an opening hidden from the sender. Messaging to extraterrestrial intelligence (METI) is judged by reach and by what a receiver must believe to act, not by fear of punishment, and a beacon deters by witnessing: the sender need not be able to strike anyone, only to tell someone who can.

</details>


### [340] [Function Name Is All You Need to Detect Blockchain Application Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12315)
*Rui Xi,Zehua Wang,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 TxLucent，一种基于交易调用轨迹中函数名序列的区块链应用攻击检测框架，借助 Transformer 学习语义，无需手工规则或源码，实现高精度实时检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 dApp 业务逻辑漏洞的区块链应用攻击造成严重经济损失。现有检测器或依赖手工规则，或需要难以获取的智能合约源码来解析攻击交易，导致其脆弱且难以实际应用。

Method: 作者论证函数名序列足以刻画交易高层语义。实证发现 424 起真实攻击事件中 98.46% 的调用轨迹可解析为函数名，而仅 74.78% 能获取源码。据此提出 TxLucent：将调用轨迹映射为函数名序列，并用 Transformer 从序列中学习应用语义，从而在不需要手写模式或源码的情况下检测攻击。

Result: 在 424 起已知事件（14,611 笔攻击交易）上，TxLucent 的漏报率为 1.56%；对以太坊 5 亿多笔良性交易估计误报率约 0.0017%；平均每笔交易分析耗时 24.90 毫秒。

Conclusion: TxLucent 克服了对源码与人工规则的依赖，兼具高检出率、极低误报与实时分析能力，支持主流区块链上的实时攻击检测。

Abstract: Blockchain application attacks, targeting business logic bugs in decentralized applications (dApps), have been an increasing concern to their developers and users, causing significant financial loss. Existing attack detectors either rely on handcrafted rules for detection, or need difficult-to-obtain smart contract source code to analyze attack transactions. This makes them brittle and inapplicable in practice. In this paper, we argue that function name sequences suffice to capture the high-level semantics of a transaction, and hence can be used to detect blockchain application attacks. Our empirical study on transactions from 424 real-world attack incidents shows that 98.46% of call traces can be resolved to function names, whereas only 74.78% invoke contracts with available source code. Based on this observation, we propose TxLucent (pronounced "translucent"), an automated framework to detect blockchain application attacks by extracting application semantics from transaction call traces. TxLucent maps call traces to function name sequences and uses a transformer to learn semantics from such sequences. Consequently, TxLucent can detect attacks without relying on hand-coded patterns or source code. Our results show that TxLucent achieves a 1.56% false negative rate on 424 known incidents with 14,611 attack transactions, and an estimated 0.0017% false positive rate for benign transactions from over 500 million transactions on the Ethereum blockchain. Finally, TxLucent takes an average of 24.90 milliseconds to analyze a transaction, thus supporting real-time attack detection on popular blockchains.

</details>


### [341] [An Evidence-First Multi-LLM Framework for Auditable Critical-Infrastructure Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12360)
*Nurjahan,Mst Eshita Khatun,Lamine Noureddine,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"证据优先"的多LLM框架，从异构基础设施文档中构建基础设施知识库（IKB）与基础设施依赖图（IDG），通过分离证据验证、本体对齐、实体消解、依赖对齐、验证、融合与人工复核等环节，实现可审计的知识构建流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施知识分散于异构、不完整且弱结构化的证据中，依赖模型难以自动构建且难以被信任。直接让LLM生成图结构存在风险：无证据支撑的关系、术语不一致、实体身份错误以及依赖端点错误。

Method: 采用证据优先的多LLM框架：多个开源权重LLM从规范化证据中独立抽取候选实体与依赖；框架将证据验证、本体接地、实体消解、依赖对齐、验证、融合与人工复核相互分离；证据支撑、本体调和、端点消解、模型一致性与人工验证保持为不同状态，同时全程保留溯源信息与未决案例；验证后的IKB再以确定性方式投影为IDG，不引入新的LLM生成知识。

Result: 在九个基础设施项目上的评估显示：实体恢复的召回率显著高于完整有向依赖的恢复率；规范化端点消解是依赖构建的主要约束；依赖层面的跨模型重叠度远低于实体层面，说明模型更多产生互不重叠的候选断言，而非稳定的多数共识。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持一种可审计的"证据—IKB—IDG"流程，其中不确定性被保留并逐步消解，而非被压缩为单一置信度或投票决策。

Abstract: Critical-infrastructure knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous, incomplete, and weakly structured evidence, making dependency models difficult to construct automatically and difficult to trust. Large language models (LLMs) can extract structured knowledge from such evidence, but direct LLM-to-graph generation risks unsupported relationships, inconsistent terminology, incorrect entity identities, and erroneous dependency endpoints. We present an evidence-first multi-LLM framework for constructing Infrastructure Knowledge Bases (IKBs) and Infrastructure Dependency Graphs (IDGs) from heterogeneous infrastructure documentation. Multiple open-weight LLMs independently extract candidate entities and dependencies from normalized evidence, after which the framework separates evidence verification, ontology grounding, entity resolution, dependency alignment, validation, fusion, and human review. Evidence support, ontology reconciliation, endpoint resolution, model agreement, and human validation remain distinct states, while provenance and unresolved cases are preserved throughout. The validated IKB is then projected deterministically into the IDG without introducing new LLM-generated knowledge. Evaluation in nine infrastructure projects shows that entity recovery achieves substantially higher recall than complete directed dependency recovery and that canonical endpoint resolution is a major constraint in dependency construction. Cross-model overlap is also much lower for dependencies than for entities, indicating that the models often produce non-overlapping candidate assertions rather than a stable majority consensus. These findings support an auditable evidence-to-IKB-to-IDG process in which uncertainty is preserved and resolved progressively rather than collapsed into a single confidence or voting decision.

</details>


### [342] [PDoS: A Profitable Denial-of-Service Attack against Proof-of-Work Blockchain Liveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12450)
*Junjie Hu,Tianzhu Han,Na Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PDoS，一种结合区块头信号威慑与寄生收益提取的混合攻击，可在补贴攻击成本的同时破坏PoW区块链活性，是首个证明破坏PoW活性可经济自持甚至盈利的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoW区块链的安全与活性依赖矿工的经济理性。既有激励驱动的DoS攻击（如BDoS）需攻击者持续承担巨额经济损失，难以在高价值网络中维持；而此前的渗透式扣块攻击旨在获利而非直接破坏链活性。因此，需要一种既能破坏活性又能自我补贴成本的攻击模型。

Method: PDoS将区块头信号威慑与寄生收益提取相结合：通过区块头信号威慑理性矿工，并利用受害矿池的份额奖励机制提取寄生收益以补贴攻击成本，从而降低诱导理性矿工停机的对抗算力阈值。文中进一步分析高手续费或高MEV环境下区块价值对攻击经济性的影响。

Result: PDoS降低了诱导理性矿工停机所需的对抗算力阈值，使攻击成本被受害矿池的份额奖励机制部分补贴。同时得到反直觉结果：在高手续费或高MEV环境中，更高的区块价值反而会提升攻击者的寄生收益，使攻击越过盈亏平衡点，进入自持甚至盈利状态，从而降低PoW系统安全性。

Conclusion: PDoS是首个表明破坏PoW区块链活性可以经济自持、甚至盈利的攻击，揭示了高手续费/高MEV环境下区块价值上升可能削弱PoW安全性的机制。

Abstract: The security and liveness of Proof-of-Work (PoW) blockchains fundamentally depend on the economic rationality of miners. Existing incentive-driven denial-of-service attacks, such as BDoS, can deter rational miners from participating, but require the attacker to continuously absorb substantial economic losses and are therefore difficult to sustain in high-value networks. Meanwhile, prior infiltration-based withholding attacks are designed to extract revenue rather than to directly disrupt chain liveness.
  We present PDoS, a hybrid attack that combines block header signal deterrence with parasitic revenue extraction. PDoS disrupts blockchain liveness while exploiting the victim pool's share-reward mechanism to subsidize the attack cost, thereby lowering the adversarial hash-power threshold required to induce rational miners to shut down. We further show a counterintuitive result: in high-fee or high-MEV environments, higher block value can make PoW systems less secure by increasing the attacker's parasitic revenue and pushing the attack across the break-even point into a self-sustaining, or even profitable, regime. To the best of our knowledge, PDoS is the first attack to demonstrate that disrupting PoW blockchain liveness can be economically self-sustaining and even profitable.

</details>


### [343] [Access Control as Verified Parse Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12488)
*Saranachon Iammongkol,Zhiyi Huang,David Eyers*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"类级"（class result）验证方法：将固定偏移字段与有界析取的访问控制决策函数编码进固定大小字节缓冲区，用 EverParse 生成无回溯验证器，并用 SMT 求解器一次性机器验证其等价性，使单次证明可覆盖该类中所有策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用安全网关在网络与策略决策之间的代码路径上反复出现实现漏洞：手写执行逻辑偏离策略作者意图，网络边界的临时请求解析器自身引入内存安全缺陷。问题根源在于已部署的执行代码而非策略本身。现有方案要么不验证执行运行时，要么仅通过差分测试把形式化模型与手写引擎相连，缺乏对执行代码本身的机器可验证保证。

Method: 将某有界策略语言的决策函数编码为固定大小的字节缓冲区，针对所有字节取值用 SMT 求解器一次性验证 EverParse 验证器：证明其当且仅当决策函数对任意策略、请求和会话均接受时接受。所依赖的 EverParse 验证器为前向、无回溯的识别器，对应一个有界有限状态类；具有固定偏移字段和有界析取的访问控制决策函数属于该类，因此一次机器检查的证明可迁移至该类内每个策略，而非逐策略重新建立。验证门与平台无关，仅需 EverParse/Z3 与 C 编译器（其正确性作为假设）。

Result: 建立了类级结论：一个前向、无回溯的 EverParse 验证器是该有界有限状态类的已验证识别器，固定偏移字段与有界析取的访问控制决策函数属于此类，故一次机器检查证明可覆盖类内所有策略。在固定端点集上编辑规则内容无需新证明；增加端点需重跑工具链；扩展语言则需新证明。在 seL4 微内核上完成部署演示，确保每个请求都经由该验证门，且未验证组件无法破坏已验证的执行链。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了策略的忠实执行（faithful enforcement），而非策略本身的安全性。其贡献在于以类级、一次验证覆盖多策略的方式，把策略决策到执行代码的等价性交由机器检查，并借助 seL4 的隔离保证执行链不可被未验证组件篡改，代价是仅支持固定偏移字段与有界析取的有界策略类，语言扩展仍需重新证明。

Abstract: Commercial security gateways repeatedly ship implementation bugs in the code path between the network and the policy decision: hand-written enforcement logic that diverges from the policy author's intent, and ad-hoc request parsers at the network boundary that introduce memory-safety flaws of their own. In both cases the bug is in the deployed enforcement code, not in the policy. Existing approaches either leave the enforcement runtime unverified or connect a formal model to a hand-written engine only by differential testing.
  Our contribution is a class result: a forward-only, backtrack-free EverParse validator is a verified recognizer for a bounded, finite-state class, and access-control decision functions with fixed-offset fields and bounded disjunction belong to it, so one machine-checked proof transfers to every policy in the class rather than being re-established per policy. Concretely, we encode a bounded policy language's decision function into a fixed-size byte buffer and verify the enforcement code once---covering all byte values---with an SMT solver, proving the validator accepts if and only if the decision function accepts, for every policy, request, and session. Editing rule content over a fixed endpoint set then needs no new proof; adding endpoints reruns the toolchain; extending the language needs new proofs. We establish faithful enforcement of a policy, not that a policy is itself secure.
  The verified gate is platform-independent, requiring only EverParse/Z3 and a C compiler, whose correctness we assume. We demonstrate a deployment on the seL4 microkernel, which ensures every request passes through the gate and that unverified components cannot corrupt the verified enforcement chain.

</details>


### [344] [Omniscience for the Masses: New Threats in the Metaverse's Democratized World Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12554)
*Andrea Mengascini,Ryan Aurelio,Jason Polakis,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对元宇宙世界创建者进行系统性安全与隐私评估，调查25个支持用户创建世界的平台，设计并实现5种新型攻击，并证明5种已有攻击可仅用标准建造功能复现，揭示现有防护不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元宇宙平台依赖用户生成的自包含虚拟世界来实现增长与创意，但这一模式将社交交互和世界行为的控制权授予不可信用户；而已有研究缺乏对"世界创建者"这一角色的系统性安全与隐私评估，且不清楚平台授予的能力是否超出用户隐私预期。

Method: 首先调查25个支持用户创建世界的平台，分析其世界创建能力；据此设计并实现5种新型攻击，利用创建者提供的工具突破沉浸式环境中的空间、视觉与听觉约束；随后复现5种既有攻击，仅使用标准世界创建功能；并评估平台的内容审核、运行时防护与创建者政策。

Result: 5种新型攻击可在无软件漏洞、无开发者级权限的情况下，在沉浸式环境中突破空间、视觉和听觉约束，实现隐蔽的用户监视与操纵；5种先前提出的攻击可仅凭标准世界创建功能复现；现有平台审核、运行时防护与创建者政策均不足以缓解恶意创建者行为。

Conclusion: 世界创建者被授予的权限与用户隐私预期之间存在根本性错配；仅靠平台现有机制无法防御恶意世界创建者，需要重新审视并约束创建者能力与政策设计。

Abstract: Metaverse platforms increasingly derive their success from user-generated virtual worlds: self-contained social and interactive environments, which can be created by any ordinary user and scale to billions of visits. Platforms such as Roblox, Horizon Worlds, and VRChat now host millions of creator-built worlds that govern how users see, hear, and interact with one another. While this model enables rapid growth and creativity, it fundamentally delegates control over social interactions and world behavior to untrusted users. In this paper, we present the first systematic security and privacy assessment of metaverse world creators. We survey 25 platforms that support user-created worlds and analyze their world-creation capabilities. Guided by this analysis, we design and implement five novel attacks that exploit creator-provided tools to violate spatial, visual, and auditory constraints in immersive environments, enabling covert user surveillance and manipulation without software vulnerabilities or developer-level privileges. We further show that five previously-proposed attacks can be replicated using only standard world-creation features. Finally, we find that existing platform vetting, runtime protections, and creator policies are insufficient to mitigate malicious world-creator behavior, revealing a fundamental mismatch between users' privacy expectations and the powers granted to world creators.

</details>


### [345] [PIA-Bench: Towards Automated Privacy Impact Assessment with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12571)
*Jiamin Zheng,Hao-Ping Lee,Luo Mai,Jingjie Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向隐私影响评估(PIA)的开源基准 PIA-Bench，用于评测大语言模型在真实 PIA 场景中的自动化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIA 是机构主动识别隐私风险并制定缓解策略的关键工具，且在监管与机构层面被强制要求；但执行 PIA 需要大量隐私与技术专业知识，对缺乏相关资源的团队尤为困难。既有工作显示 LLM 可辅助隐私决策，但其能否准确、可靠地自动化 PIA 尚不明确。

Method: 审计美国联邦机构发布的 499 份专家撰写的 PIA，筛选整理出 73 份结构化 PIA，共包含 451 条隐私风险项与 831 条缓解措施项，构建 PIA-Bench 基准，用于评估 LLM 评估复杂系统隐私风险与提出缓解措施的能力。

Result: 现有开箱即用的 LLM 能产生有意义的评估结果，同时暴露出可改进的方向（具体量化表现未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁改进面向 LLM 智能体的领域特定工作流、建设可问责的 LLM 基础设施，并为 PIA 设计新的质量标准。

Abstract: Privacy impact assessment (PIA) is a critical instrument for institutions to proactively identify privacy risks and develop mitigation strategies before system deployment. While mandated across regulatory and institutional contexts, executing PIA requires extensive privacy and technical expertise, posing a particular challenge for teams without access to such resources. Prior work shows the potential of leveraging large language models (LLMs) to assist practitioners' privacy decisions, but little is known about how accurately and reliably LLMs can automate PIA. To this end, we develop PIA-Bench, the first open benchmark for evaluating LLMs on real-world PIAs. We first audited 499 expert-authored PIAs published by US federal agencies and curated 73 structured PIAs, comprising a total of 451 privacy risk and 831 mitigation items, to evaluate LLMs' ability to assess privacy risks and propose mitigations of complex systems. Our results show that off-the-shelf LLMs produce meaningful assessments and identify avenues for future improvement. Finally, we call for improving domain-specific workflows for LLM agents, developing accountable LLM infrastructure, and designing new quality standards for PIAs.

</details>
